        <body>

            <div n="4" type="sthāna">

                <head>cikitsāsthānaṃ</head>

                <div n="1" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>prathamo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <pb facs="480" n="396a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.1">
                        <l> athāto dvivraṇīyaṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.3">
                        <l> dvau vraṇau bhavataḥ śārīra, āgantuś ca | tayoḥ śārīraḥ
                            pavanapittakaphaśoṇitasannipātanimittaḥ; āgantur api puruṣa<pc
                                > </pc>paśu<pc> </pc>pakṣi<pc> </pc>vyāla<pc
                                > </pc>sarīsṛpa<pc> </pc>prapatanapīḍana<pc
                                > </pc>prahārāgnikṣāra<pc> </pc>viṣa<pc> </pc>tīkṣṇauṣadha<pc
                                > </pc>śakala<pc
                                > </pc>kapālaśṛṅgackreṣuparaśuśaktikuntādyāyudhābhighāta<pc
                            > </pc>nimittaḥ | tatra tulye vraṇasāmānye
                            dvikāraṇotthānaprayojanasāmarthyād dvivraṇīya ity ucyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.4">
                        <l> sarvasminn evāgantuvraṇe tatkālam eva kṣatoṣmaṇaḥ prasṛtasyopaśamārthaṃ
                            ceti śleṣmaśoṇitābhyāṃ, sphuraṇatodadāhadhūmāyanaprāyaḥ
                            pītatanuraktasrāvī ceti vātapittaśoṇitebhyaḥ,
                            kaṇḍūsphuraṇacumacumāyamānaprāyaḥ pāṇḍughanaraktāsrāvī ceti
                            vātaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ, dāhapākarāgakaṇḍūprāyaḥ pāṇḍughanaraktāsrāvī ceti
                            pittaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ, trividhavarṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ
                            pavanapittakaphebhyaḥ,
                            nirdahananirmathanasphuraṇatodadāhapākarāgakaṇḍūsvāpabahulo
                            nānāvarṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ pavanapittakaphaśoṇitebhyaḥ,
                            jihvātalābho mṛduḥ snigdhaḥ ślakṣṇo vigatavedanaḥ suvyavasthito
                            nirāsrāvaś ceti śuddho vraṇa iti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="480" n="396b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.5">
                        <l> doṣopaplavaviśeṣaḥ punaḥ samāsataḥ pañcadaśaprakāraḥ,
                            prasaraṇasāmarthyāt, yathokto vraṇapraśnādhikāre; śuddhatvāt
                            ṣoḍaśaprakāra ity eke|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.6">
                        <l> tasya lakṣaṇaṃ dvividhaṃ sāmānyaṃ, vaiśeṣikaṃ ca| tatra sāmānyaṃ ruk|
                            vraṇa gātravicūrṇane, vraṇayatīti vraṇaḥ| viśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ
                            punarvātādiliṅgaviśeṣaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.7">
                        <l> tatra śyāvāruṇābhastanuḥ śītaḥ picchilo 'lpasrāvī
                            rūkṣaścaṭacaṭāyanaśīlaḥ sphuraṇāyāmatodabhedavedanābahulo nirmāṃsaś ceti
                            vātāt, kṣiprajaḥ pīta<pb facs="481" n="397a"/>nīlābhaḥ
                            kiṃśukodakābhoṣṇasrāvī dāhapākarāgavikārakārī pītapiḍakājuṣṭaśceti
                            pittāt, pratatacaṇḍakaṇḍūbahulaḥ sthūlauṣṭhaḥ
                            stabdhasirāsnāyujālāvatataḥ kaṭhinaḥ pāṇḍvavabhāso mandavedanaḥ
                            śuklaśītasāndrapicchilāsrāvī guruśceti kaphāt, pravāladalanicayaprakāśaḥ
                            kṛṣṇasphoṭapiḍākājālopacitasturaṅgasthānagandhiḥ savedano dhūmāyanaśīlo
                            raktasrāvī pittaliṅgaśceti raktāt, todadāhadhūmāyanaprāyaḥ
                            pītāruṇābhastadvarṇasrāvī ceti vātapittābhyāṃ, kaṇḍūyanaśīlaḥ sanistodo
                            rūkṣo gururdāruṇo muhurmuhuḥ śītapicchilālpasrāvī ceti vātaśleṣmabhyāṃ,
                            guruḥ sadāha uṣṇaḥ pītapāṇḍusrāvī ceti pittaśleṣmabhyāṃ,
                            rūkṣastanustodabahulaḥ supta iva ca raktāruṇābhastadvarṇāsrāvī ceti
                            vātaśoṇitābhyāṃ, ghṛtamaṇḍābho
                            mīnadhāvanatoyagandhirmṛdurvisarpyuṣṇakṛṣṇasrāvī ceti pittaśoṇitābhyāṃ,
                            rakto guruḥ snigdhaḥ picchilaḥ kaṇḍūprāyaḥ sthiro saraktapāṇḍusrāvī ceti
                            śleṣmaśoṇitābhyāṃ, sphuraṇatodadāhadhūmāyanaprāyaḥ pītatanuraktasrāvī
                            ceti vātapittaśoṇitebhyaḥ, kaṇḍūsphuraṇacumacumāyamānaprāyaḥ
                            pāṇḍughanaraktāsrāvī ceti vātaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ, dāhapākarāgakaṇḍūprāyaḥ
                            pāṇḍughanaraktāsrāvī ceti pittaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ,
                            trividhavarṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ pavanapittakaphebhyaḥ,
                            nirdahananirmathanasphuraṇatodadāhapākarāgakaṇḍūsvāpabahulo
                            nānāvarṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ pavanapittakaphaśoṇitebhyaḥ,
                            jihvātalābho mṛduḥ snigdhaḥ ślakṣṇo vigatavedanaḥ suvyavasthito
                            nirāsrāvaśceti śuddho vraṇa iti|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="481" n="397b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.8">
                        <l> tasya vraṇasya paṣṭirupakramā bhavanti | tad yathā apatarpaṇam ālepaḥ
                            pariṣeko 'bhyaṅgaḥ svedo vimpālanam upanāhaḥ pācanaṃ visrāvaṇaṃ sneho
                            vamanaṃ virecanaṃ chedanaṃ bhedanaṃ dāraṇaṃ lekhanam eṣaṇam āharaṇaṃ
                            vyadhanaṃ visrāvaṇaṃ sīvanaṃ sandhānaṃ pīḍanaṃ śoṇitāsthāpanaṃ
                            nirvāpaṇam utkārikā kaṣāyo vartiḥ kalkaḥ sarpistailaṃ rasakriyā
                            'vacūrṇanaṃ vraṇadhūpanam utsādanam avasādanaṃ mṛdukarma dāruṇakarma
                            kṣārakarmāgnikarma kṛṣṇakarma pāṇḍukarma pratisāraṇaṃ romasañjananaṃ
                            lomāpaharaṇaṃ bastikarmottarabastikarma bandhaḥ patradānaṃ kṛmighnaṃ
                            bṛṃhaṇaṃ viṣaghnaṃ śirovirecanaṃ nasyaṃ kavaladhāraṇaṃ dhūmo madhusārpir
                            yantram āhāro rakṣāvidhānam iti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.9">
                        <l> teṣu kaṣāyo vartiḥ kalkaḥ sarpistailaṃ rasakriyā 'vacūrṇanam iti
                            śodhanaropaṇāni, teṣv aṣṭau śastrakṛtyāḥ, śoṇitāsthāpanaṃ kṣāro 'gnir
                            yantram ahāro rakṣāvidhānaṃ bandhavidhānaṃ coktāni, snehasvedana<pb
                                facs="482" n="398a"
                            />vamanavirecanabastyuttarabastiśirovirecananasyadhūmakavalaghāraṇānyanyatra
                            vakṣyāmaḥ, yad anyad avaśiṣṭam upakramajātaṃ tad iha vakṣyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.10">
                        <l> ṣaḍvidhaḥ prāgupadiṣṭaḥ śophaḥ, tasyaikādaśopakramā bhavanty
                            apatarpaṇādayo virecanāntāḥ; te ca viśeṣeṇa śothapratīkāre vartante,
                            vraṇabhāvam āpannasya ca na virudhyante; śeṣās tu prāyeṇa
                            vraṇapratīkārahetava eva || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.11">
                        <l> apatarpaṇamādya upakramaḥ; eṣa sarvaśophānāṃ sāmānyaḥ pradhānatamaś ca
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.12">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra doṣocchrāyopaśāntyarthaṃ doṣānaddhasya dehinaḥ | avekṣya
                            doṣaṃ prāṇaṃ ca kāryaṃ syād apatarpaṇam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.13">
                        <l> ūrdhvamārutatṛṣṇākṣunmukhaśoṣaśramānvitaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             na kāryaṃ garbhiṇīvṛddhabāladurbalabhīrubhiḥ dhyāme sarakte vedanāvati
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.14">
                        <l> śopheṣūtthitamātreṣu vraṇeṣūgrarujeṣu ca| yathāsvair auṣadhair lepaṃ
                            pratyekaśyena kārayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.15">
                        <l> yathā pravajvalite veśmanyambhasā paribecanam| kṣipraṃ
                            praśamayatyagnimevam ālepanaṃ rujaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.16">
                        <l> prahlādane śodhane ca śophasya haraṇe tathā| utsādane ropaṇe ca lepaḥ
                            syāt tu tadarthakṛt|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="483" n="399a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.17">
                        <l> vātaśophe tu vedanopaśamārthaṃ
                            sarpistailadhānyāmlamāṃsarasavātaharauṣadhaniṣkvāthair aśītaiḥ pariṣekān
                            kurvīta, pittaraktābhighātaviṣanimitteṣu
                            kṣīraghṛtamadhuśarkarodakekṣurasamadhurauṣadhakṣīravṛkṣaniṣkvāthair
                            anuṣṇaiḥ pariṣekān kurvīta, śleṣmaśophe tu
                            tailamūtrakṣārodakasurāśuktakaphaghnauṣadhaniṣkvāthair aśītaiḥ pariṣekān
                            kurvīta|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.18">
                        <l> yathā+ambubhiḥ sicyamānaḥ śāntimagnirniyacchati| doṣāgnirevaṃ sahasā
                            pariṣekeṇa śāmyati|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.19">
                        <l> abhyaṅgas tu doṣamālokyopayukto doṣopaśamaṃ mṛdutāṃ ca karoti|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.20">
                        <l> svedavimlāpanādīnāṃ kriyāṇāṃ prāk sa ucyate| pañcāt karmasu cādiṣṭaḥ sa
                            ca visrāvaṇādiṣu|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.21">
                        <l> rujāvatāṃ dāruṇānāṃ kaṭhinānāṃ tathaiva ca| śophānāṃ svedanaṃ kāryaṃ ye
                            cāpy evaṃvidhā vraṇāḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.22">
                        <l> sthirāṇāṃ rujatāṃ mandaṃ kāryaṃ vimlāpanaṃ bhavet| abhyajya svedayitvā
                            tu veśunāḍyā tataḥ śanaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.23">
                        <l> vimardayed bhiṣak prājñastalenāṅguṣṭhakena vā| śophayor upanāhaṃ tu
                            kuryād āmavidagdhayoḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.24">
                        <l> avidagdhaḥ śamaṃ yāti vidagdhaḥ pākameti ca| nivartate na yaḥ śopho
                            virekāntair upakramaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.25">
                        <l> tasya saṃpācanaṃ kuryāt samāhṛtyauṣadhāni tu|
                            dadhitakrasurāśuktadhānyāmlair yojitāni tu|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.26">
                        <l> snigdhāni lavaṇīkṛtya paced utkārikāṃ śubhām| sairaṇḍapatrayā śophaṃ
                            nāhayed uṣṇayā tayā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.27">
                        <l> hitaṃ sambhojanaṃ cāpi pākāyābhimukho yadi| vedanopaśamārthāya tathā
                            pākaśamāya ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.28">
                        <l> acirotpatite śophe kuryāc choṇitamokṣaṇam| saśophe kaṭhine dhyāme
                            sarakte vedanāvati|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.29">
                        <l> saṃrabdhe viṣame cāpi vraṇe visrāvaṇaṃ hitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             saviṣe ca viśeṣeṇa jalaukobhiḥ padais tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.30">
                        <l> vedanāyāḥ praśāntyarthaṃ pākasyāprāptaye tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             sopadravāṇāṃ rūkṣāṇāṃ kṛśānāṃ vraṇaśoṣiṇām || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.31">
                        <l> yathāsvamauṣadhaiḥ siddhaṃ snehapānaṃ vidhīyate |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="484" n="400a"/> utsannamāṃsaśophe tu kaphajuṣṭe viśeṣataḥ ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.32">
                        <l> saṃkliṣṭaśyā(dhyā)marudhire vraṇe pracchardanaṃ hitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             vātapittapraduṣṭeṣu dīrghakālānubandhiṣu || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.33">
                        <l> virecanaṃ praśaṃsanti vraṇeṣu vraṇakovidāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             apākeṣu tu rogeṣu kaṭhineṣu sthireṣu ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.34">
                        <l> snāyukothādiṣu tathā cchedanaṃ prāptamucyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             antaḥpūyeṣv avaktreṣu tathaivotsaṅgavatsv api|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.35">
                        <l> gatimatsu ca rogeṣu bhedanaṃ prāptam ucyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             bālavṛddhāsahakṣīṇabhīrūṇāṃ yoṣitām api || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.36">
                        <l> marmopari ca jāteṣu rogeṣūkteṣu dāraṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             supakve piṇḍite śophe pīḍanair upapīḍite || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.37">
                        <l> pākodvṛtteṣu doṣeṣu tat tu kāryaṃ vijānatā |
                            <caesura/>
                             supiṣṭair dāraṇadravyair yuktaiḥ kṣāreṇa vā punaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.38">
                        <l> kaṭhinān sthūlavṛttauṣṭhān dīryamāṇān punaḥ punaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kaṭhinotsannamāṃsāṃś ca lesvanenācared bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.39">
                        <l> samaṃ likhet sulikhitaṃ likhenniravaśeṣataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             vartmanāṃ tu pramāṇena samaṃ śastreṇa nirlikhet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.40">
                        <l> kṣaumaṃ plotaṃ picuṃ phenaṃ yāvaśūkaṃ sasaindhavam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kakaśāni ca patrāṇi lekhanārthe pradāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.41">
                        <l> nāḍīvraṇāñ śalyagarbhānunmārgyutsaṅginaḥ śanaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             karīrabālāṅgulibhir eṣaṇyā vaiṣayed bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.42">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.120">
                        <l> tīvrā rujo vicitrāś ca raktāsrāvaś ca jāyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             surasādirhitas tatra dhāvane pūraṇe tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.121">
                        <l> saptaparṇakarañjārkanimbarājādanatvacaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             hitā gomūtrapiṣṭāś ca sekaḥ kṣārodakena vā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.122">
                        <l> pracchādya māṃsapeśyā vā kṛmīn apahared vraṇāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             viṃśatiṃ kṛmijātīs tu vaksyāmyupari bhāgaśaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.123">
                        <l> dīrghakālāturāṇāṃ tu kṛśānāṃ vraṇaśoṣiṇām |
                            <caesura/>
                             bṛṃhaṇīyo vidhiḥ sarvaḥ kāyāgniṃ parirakṣatā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.124">
                        <l> viṣajuṣṭasya vijñānāṃ viṣaniścayam eva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             cikitsitaṃ ca vakṣyāmi kalpeṣu pratibhāgaśaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="491" n="407a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.125">
                        <l> kaṇḍūmantaḥ saśophāś ca ye ca jatrūpari vraṇāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             śirovirecanaṃ teṣu vidadhyātkuśalo bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.126">
                        <l> rujāvanto 'nilāviṣṭā rūkṣā ye cordhvajatrujāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             vraṇeṣu teṣu kartavyaṃ nasyaṃ vaidyena jānatā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.127">
                        <l> doṣapracyāvanārthāya rujādāhakṣayāya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             jihvādantasamutthasya haraṇārthaṃ malasya ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.128">
                        <l> śodhano ropaṇaś caiva vraṇasya mukhajasya vai |
                            <caesura/>
                             uṣṇo vā yadi vā śītaḥ kavalagraha iṣyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.129">
                        <l> ūrdhvajatrugatān rogān vraṇāṃś ca kaphavātajān |
                            <caesura/>
                             śophasrāvarujāyuktān dhūmapānair upācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.130">
                        <l> kṣatoṣmaṇo nigrahārthaṃ sandhānārthaṃ tathaiva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             sadyovraṇeṣv āyateṣu kṣaudrasarpir vidhīyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.131">
                        <l> avagāḍhāstvaṇumukhā ye vraṇāḥ śalyapīḍitāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             nivṛttahastoddharaṇā yantraṃ teṣu vidhīyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.132">
                        <l> laghumātro laghuś caiva snigdha uṣṇo 'gnidīpanaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarvavraṇibhyo deyas tu sadā ''āhāro vijānatā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.133">
                        <l> niśācarebhyo rakṣyas tu nityam eva kṣatāturaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             rakṣāvidhānair uddiṣṭair yamaiḥ saniyamais tatahā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="491" n="407b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.134">
                        <l> ṣaṇmūlo 'ṣṭaparigrāhī pañcalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             ṣaṣṭyā cidhānair nirdiṣṭaiś caturbhiḥ sādhyate vraṇaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.135">
                        <l> yo 'lpauṣadhakṛto yogo bahugranthabhayānmayā |
                            <caesura/>
                             dravyāṇāṃ tatsamānānāṃ tatrāvāpo na duṣyati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.136">
                        <l> prasaṅgābhihito yo vā bahudurlabhabheṣajaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             yathopapatti tatrāpi kāryam eva cikitsitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="492" n="408a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.137">
                        <l> goṇoktam api yaddravyaṃ bhaved vyādhāvayaugikam |
                            <caesura/>
                             tad uddhared yaugikaṃ tu prakṣiped apy akīrtitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.138">
                        <l> upadravās tu vividhā vraṇasya vraṇitasya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatra gandhādayaḥ pañca vraṇasyopadravāḥ smṛtāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.139">
                        <l> jvarātisārau mūrcchā ca hikkā cchardirarocakaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             śvāsakāsāvipākāś ca tṛṣṇā ca vraṇitasya tu || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.140">
                        <l> vraṇakriyāsvevamāsu vyāsenoktāsv apikriyām |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhūyo 'pyupari vakṣyāmi sadyovraṇacikitsite ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne dvivraṇīyacikitsitaṃ nāma prathamo
                            'dhyāyaḥ ||1 ||</l>
                    </lg>
                </div>

                <div n="2" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.1">
                        <l> athātaḥ sadyovraṇacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.3">
                        <l> dhanvantarir dharmabhṛtāṃ variṣṭho vāgviśāradaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśvāmitrasutaṃ śiṣyam ṛṣiṃ suśrutam anvaśāt || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.4">
                        <l> nānādhārāmukhaiḥ śastrair nānāsthānanipātitaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             nānārūpā vraṇā ye syus teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.5">
                        <l> āyatāś caturasrāś ca tryasrā maṇḍalinas tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             ardhacandrapratīkāśā viśālāḥ kuṭilās tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.6">
                        <l> śarāvanimnamadhyāś ca yavamadhyās tathā 'pare |
                            <caesura/>
                             evaṃprakārākṛtayo bhavanty āgantavo vraṇāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.7">
                        <l> doṣajā vā svayaṃ bhinnā na tu vaidyanimittajāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhiṣagvraṇākṛtijño hi na moham adhigacchati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.8">
                        <l> bhṛśaṃ durdarśarūpeṣu vraṇeṣu vikṛteṣv api |
                            <caesura/>
                             anantākṛtir āgantuḥ sa bhiṣagbhiḥ purātanaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.9">
                        <l> samāsato lakṣaṇataḥ ṣaḍvidhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             chinnaṃ bhinnaṃ tathā viddhaṃ kṣataṃ piccitam eva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.10">
                        <l> ghṛṣṭam āhus tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             tiraścīna ṛjurvā 'pi yo vraṇaś cāyato bhavet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.11">
                        <l> gātrasya pātanaṃ cāpi chinnam ity upadiśyate |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="493" n="409a"/> kuntaśaktyṛṣṭikhaṅgāgraviṣāṇādibhir āśayaḥ ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.12">
                        <l> hataḥ kiñcit sravet tad dhi bhinnalakṣaṇam ucyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             sthānāny āmāgnipakvānāṃ mūtrasya rudhirasya ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.13">
                        <l> hṛduṇḍukaḥ phupphusaś ca koṣṭha ity abhidhīyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             tasmin bhinne raktapūrṇe jvaro dāhaś ca jāyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.14">
                        <l> mūtramārgagudāsyebhyo raktaṃ ghrāṇāc ca gacchati |
                            <caesura/>
                             mūrcchāśvāsatṛḍādhmānamabhaktacchanda eva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.15">
                        <l> viṇmūtravātasaṅgaś ca svedāsrāvo 'kṣiraktatā |
                            <caesura/>
                             lohagandhitvam āsyasya gātradaurgandhyam eva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.16">
                        <l> hṛcchūlaṃ pārśvayoś cāpi viśeṣaṃ cātra me śṛṇu |
                            <caesura/>
                             āmāśayasthe rudhire rudhiraṃ saṃgṛhya samutthāpya mahābalāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhavaty antaḥpraveśas tu yathā nirdhunuyus tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.17">
                        <l> ādhmānamatimātraṃ ca śūlaṃ ca bhṛśadāruṇam| pakvāśayagate cāpi rujo
                            gauravam eva ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.18">
                        <l> śītatā cāpy adho nāmeḥ khebhyo raktasya cāgamaḥ|
                            abhinne+apyāśaye+antrāṇāṃ khaiḥ sūkṣmair antrapūraṇam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.19">
                        <l> pihitāsye ghaṭe yadvallakṣyate tasya gauravam| sūkṣmāsyaśalyābhihataṃ
                            yadaṅgaṃ tvāśayādvinā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.20">
                        <l> uttuṇḍitaṃ nirgataṃ vā tadviddham iti nirdiśet| nāticchinnaṃ
                            nātibhinnamubhayor lakṣaṇānvitam|| </l>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.21">
                        <l> viṣamaṃ vraṇamaṅge yattat kṣataṃ tvabhinirdiśet| prahārapīḍanābhyāṃ tu
                            yadaṅgaṃ pṛthutāṃ gatam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.22">
                        <l> sāsthi tat piccitaṃ vidyānmajjaraktapariplutam| vigatatvagyadaṅgaṃ hi
                            saṃgharṣādanyathā+api vā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.23">
                        <l> uṣāsrāvānvitaṃ tattu ghṛṣṭamity upadiśyate| chinne bhinne tathā viddhe
                            kṣate vā+asṛgatisravet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.24">
                        <l> raktakṣayādrujas tatra karoti pavano bhṛśam| snehapānaṃ hitaṃ tatra
                            tatseko vihitastathā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.25">
                        <l> veśavāraiḥ sakṛśaraiḥ susnigdhaiścopanāhanam| dhānyasvedāṃś ca kurvīta
                            snigdhānyālepanāni ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.26">
                        <l> vātaghnauṣadhasiddhaiś ca snehair bastirvidhīyate| piccite ca vighṛṣṭe
                            ca nātisravapi śoṇitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.27">
                        <l> agacchati bhṛśaṃ tasmin dāhaḥ pākaś ca jāyate| tatroṣmaṇo nigrahārthaṃ
                            tathā dāhaprapākayoḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.28">
                        <l> śītam ālepanaṃ kāryaṃ pariṣekaś ca śītalaḥ| <pb facs="494" n="410a"/>
                            ṣaṭsveteṣu yathokteṣu chinnādiṣu samāsataḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.29">
                        <l> jñeyaṃ samarpitaṃ sarvaṃ sadyovraṇacikitsitam| ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi
                            chinnānāṃ tu cikitsitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.30">
                        <l> ye vraṇā vivṛtāḥ kecicchiraḥpārśvāvalambinaḥ| tān sīvyed vidhinoktena
                            badhnīyād gāḍham eva ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.31">
                        <l> karṇaṃ sthānādapahṛtaṃ sthāpayitvā yathāsthitam| sīvyed yathoktaṃ
                            tailena srotaś cābhipratarpayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.32">
                        <l> kṛkāṭikānte chinne tu gacchatyapi samīraṇe| samyaṅniveśya badhnīyāt
                            sīvyec cāpi nirantaram|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.33">
                        <l> ājena sapiṣā caivaṃ pariṣekaṃ tu kārayet| uttāno 'nnaṃ samaśnīyācchayīta
                            ca suyantritaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.34">
                        <l> śākhāsu patitāṃstiryak prahārān vivṛtān bhṛśam| sīvyet samyaṅniveśyāśu
                            sandhyasthīnyanupūrvaśaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.35">
                        <l> baddhvā vellitakenāśu tatastailena secayet| carmaṇā gophaṇābandhaḥ kāryo
                            yo vā hito bhavet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.36">
                        <l> pṛṣṭhe vraṇo yasya bhaved uttānaṃ śāyayet tu tam| ato 'nyathā corasije
                            śāyayet puruṣaṃ vraṇe|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.37">
                        <l> chinnāṃ niḥśeṣataḥ śākhāṃ dagdhvā tailena buddhimān| badhnīyāt
                            kośabandhena prāptaṃ kāryaṃ ca ropaṇam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.38">
                        <l> candanaṃ padmakaṃ rodhramutpalāni priyaṅgavaḥ| haridrā madhukaṃ caiva
                            payaḥ syādatra cāṣṭamam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.39">
                        <l> tailamebhir vipakvaṃ tu pradhānaṃ vraṇaropaṇam| candanaṃ karkaṭākhyā ca
                            sahe māṃsyāhvayā+amṛtā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.40">
                        <l> hareṇavo mṛṇālaṃ ca triphalā padmakotpale| trayodaśāṅgaṃ trivṛtametadvā
                            payasā+anvitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.41">
                        <l> tailaṃ vipakvaṃ sekārthe hitaṃ tu vraṇaropaṇe| ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi
                            bhinnānāṃ tu cikitsitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.42">
                        <l> bhinnaṃ netramakarmaṇyamabhinnaṃ lambate tu yat| tanniveśya
                            yathāsthānamavyāviddhasiraṃ śanaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.43">
                        <l> pīḍayet pāṇinā samyak padmapattrāntareṇa tu| tato 'sya tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ
                            nasyaṃ cānena sarpiṣā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="495" n="411a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.44">
                        <l> ājaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīrapātraṃ madhukaṃ cotpalāni ca| jīvakarṣabhakau caiva
                            piṣṭvā sarpir vipācayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.45">
                        <l> sarvanetrābhighāte tu sarpiretat praśasyate| udarānmedaso vartirnirgatā
                            yasya dehinaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.46">
                        <l> kaṣāyabhasmamṛtkīrṇāṃ baddhvā sūtreṇa sūtravit| agnitaptena śastreṇa
                            chindyānmadhusamāyutam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.47">
                        <l> baddhvā vraṇaṃ sujīrṇe+anne sarpiṣaḥ pānamiṣyate| snehapānādṛte cāpi
                            payaḥpānaṃ vidhīyate|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.48">
                        <l> śarkarāmadhuyaṣṭibhyāṃ lākṣayā vā śvadaṃṣṭrayā| citrāsamanvitaṃ caiva
                            rujādāhavināśanam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.49">
                        <l> āṭopo maraṇaṃ vā syācchūlo vā+acchidyamānayā| medogranthau tu yattailaṃ
                            vakṣyate tac ca yojayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.50">
                        <l> tvaco 'tītya sirādīni bhittvā vā parihṛtya vā| koṣṭhe pratiṣṭhitaṃ
                            śalyaṃ kuryād uktānupadravān|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.51">
                        <l> tatrāntarlohitaṃ pāṇḍuṃ śītapādakarānanam| śītocchvāsaṃ
                            raktanetramānaddhaṃ ca vivarjayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.52">
                        <l> āmāśayasthe rudhire vamanaṃ pathyamucyate| pakvāśayasthe deyaṃ ca
                            virecanamasaṃśayam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.53">
                        <l> āsthāpanaṃ ca niḥshehaṃ kāryamuṣṇair viśodhanaiḥ| yavakolakulatthānāṃ
                            niḥsnehena rasena ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.54">
                        <l> bhuñjītānnaṃ yavāgūṃ vā pibet saindhavasaṃyutām| atiniḥsrutarakto vā
                            bhinnakoṣṭhaḥ pibed asṛk|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.55">
                        <l>
                            <pb facs="496" n="412a"/> svamārgapratipannās tu yasya viṇmūtramārutāḥ|
                            vyupadravaḥ sa bhinne+api koṣṭhe jīvati mānavaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.56">
                        <l> abhinnamantraṃ niṣkrāntaṃ praveśyaṃ nānyathā bhavet| pipīlikāśirograstaṃ
                            tadapyeke vadanti tu|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.57">
                        <l> prakṣālya payasā digdhaṃ tṛṇśoṇitapāṃśubhiḥ| praveśayet kṛttanakho
                            ghṛtenāktaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.58">
                        <l> praveśayet kṣīrasiktaṃ śuṣkamatraṃ ghṛtāplutam| aṅgulyā+abhimṛśet
                            kaṇṭhaṃ jalenodvejayed api|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.59">
                        <l> hastapādeṣu saṃgṛhya samutthāpya mahābalāḥ| bhavaty antaḥpraveśas tu
                            yathā nirdhunuyustathā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.60">
                        <l> tathā 'ntrāṇi viśantyantaḥ svāṃ kalāṃ pīḍayanti ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             vraṇālpatvād bahutvād vā duṣpraveśaṃ bhavet tu yat || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.61">
                        <l> tadāpāṭhya pramāṇena bhiṣagantraṃ parveśayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             yathāsthānaṃ niveṣṭe ca vraṇaṃ sīvyed atandriyaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.62">
                        <l> sthānādapetamādatte prāṇān gupitam eva vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             veṣṭayitvā tu paṭṭena ghṛtasekaṃ pradāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.63">
                        <l> ghṛtaṃ pibet sukhoṣṇaṃ ca citrātailasamanvitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             mṛdukriyārthaṃ śakṛto vāyoś cādhaḥpravṛttaye || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.64">
                        <l> tatastailamidaṃ kuryād ropaṇārthaṃ cikitsakaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tvaco 'śvakarṇadhavayor mocakīmeṣaśṛṅgayoḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.65">
                        <l> śallakyarjunayoś cāpi vidāryāḥ kṣīriṇāṃ tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             balāmūlāni cāhṛtya tailametair vipācayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.66">
                        <l> vraṇaṃ saṃropayet tena varṣamātraṃ yateta ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             pādau nirastamuṣkasya jalena prokṣya cākṣiṇī || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.67">
                        <l> praveśya tunnasevanyā muṣkau sīvyet tataḥ param |
                            <caesura/>
                             kāryo gophaṇikābandhaḥ kaṭyām āveśya yantrakam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.68">
                        <l> na kuryāt snehasekaṃ ca tena klidyati hi vraṇaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                            <caesura/>
                             kālānusāryāgurvelājātīcandanapadmakaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.69">
                        <l> śilādārvyamṛtātutthaistailaṃ kurvīta ropaṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             śiraso 'apahṛte śalye bālavartiṃ niveśayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.70">
                        <l> bālavartyām adattāyāṃ mastuluṅgaṃ vraṇāt sravet |
                            <caesura/>
                             hanyād enaṃ tato vāyus tasmād evam upācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="497" n="413a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.71">
                        <l> vraṇe rohati caikaikaṃ śanair bālamapakṣipet |
                            <caesura/>
                             gātrādapahṛte 'nyasmāt snehavartiṃ praveśayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.72">
                        <l> kṛte niḥśoṇite cāpi vidhiḥ sadyaḥkṣate hitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             dūrābagāḍhāḥ sūkṣmāḥ syurye vraṇāstān viśoṇitān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.73">
                        <l> kṛtvā sūkṣmeṇa netreṇa cakratailena tarpayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             samaṅgāṃ rajanīṃ padmāṃ trivargaṃ tutthameva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.74">
                        <l> viḍaṅgaṃ kaṭukāṃ pathyāṃ guḍūcīṃ sakarañjikām |
                            <caesura/>
                             saṃhṛtya vipacet kāle tailaṃ ropaṇamuttamam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.75">
                        <l> tālīśaṃ padmakaṃ māṃsī hareṇvagurucandanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             haridre padmabījāni sośīraṃ madhukaṃ ca taiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.76">
                        <l> pakvaṃ sadyovraṇeṣūktaṃ tailaṃ ropaṇamuttamam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣate kṣatavidhiḥ kāryaḥ piccite bhagnavadvidhiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.77">
                        <l> ghṛṣṭe rujo nigṛhyāśu cūrṇair upacared vraṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśliṣṭadehaṃ patitaṃ mathitaṃ hatam eva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.78">
                        <l> vāsayet tailapūrṇāyāṃ droṇyāṃ māṃsarasāśanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             ayam eva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ kṣīṇe marmahate tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.79">
                        <l> ropaṇe saparīṣeke pāne ca vraṇināṃ sadā |
                            <caesura/>
                             tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā saṃyojyaṃ śarīrartūnavekṣya hi || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.80">
                        <l> ghṛtāni yāni vakṣyāmi yatnataḥ pittavidradhau |
                            <caesura/>
                             sadyovraṇeṣu deyāni tāni vaidyena jānatā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.81">
                        <l> sadyaḥkṣatavraṇaṃ vaidyaḥ saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarpiṣā nātiśītena balātailena vā punaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="498" n="414a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.82">
                        <l> samaṅgāṃ rajanīṃ padmāṃ pathyāṃ tutthaṃ suvarcalām |
                            <caesura/>
                             padmakaṃ rodhramadhukaṃ viḍaṅgāni hareṇukām || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.83">
                        <l> tālīśapatraṃ naladaṃ candanaṃ padmakeśaram |
                            <caesura/>
                             mañjiṣṭhośīralākṣāś ca kṣīriṇāṃ cāpi pallavān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.84">
                        <l> priyālabījaṃ tindukyāstaruṇāni phalāni ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             yathālābhaṃ samāhṛtya tailamebhir vipācayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.85">
                        <l> sadyovraṇānāṃ sarveṣāmaduṣṭānāṃ tu ropaṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kaṣāyamadhurāḥ śītāḥ kriyāḥ snigdhāś ca yojayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.86">
                        <l> sadyovraṇānāṃ saptāhaṃ paścāt pūrvoktamācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             duṣṭavraṇeṣu kartavyamūrdhvaṃ cādhaś ca śodhanam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.87">
                        <l> viśoṣaṇaṃ tathā++āhāraḥ śoṇitasya ca mokṣaṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kaṣāyaṃ rājavṛkṣādau surasādau ca dhāvanam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.88">
                        <l> tayoreva kaṣāyeṇa tailaṃ śodhanamiṣyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣārakalpena vā tailaṃ kṣāradravyeṣu sādhitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.89">
                        <l> dravantī cirabilvaś ca dantī citrakameva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             pṛthvīkā nimbapatrāṇi kāsīsaṃ tutthameva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.90">
                        <l> trivṛttejovatī nīlī haridre saindhavaṃ tilāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhūmikadambaḥ suvahā śukākhyā lāṅgalāhvayā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.91">
                        <l> naipālī jālinī caiva madayantī mṛgādanī |
                            <caesura/>
                             sudhāmūrvārkakīṭāriharitālakarañjikāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.92">
                        <l> yathopapatti kartavyaṃ tailam etais tu śodhanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             ghṛtaṃ vā yadi vā prāptaṃ kalkāḥ saṃśodhanāstathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.93">
                        <l> saindhavatrivṛderaṇḍapatrakalkastu vātike |
                            <caesura/>
                             trivṛddharidrāmadhukakalkaḥ paitte tilair yutaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.94">
                        <l> kaphaje tilatejohvādantīsvarjikacitrakāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             duṣṭavraṇavidhiḥ kāryo mehakuṣṭhavraṇeṣvapi || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.95">
                        <l> ṣaḍvidhaḥ prāk pradiṣṭo yaḥ sadyovraṇaviniścayaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             nātaḥ śakyaṃ paraṃ vaktumapi niścitavādibhiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.96">
                        <l> upasargair nipātaiś ca tat tu paṇḍitamāninaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kecit saṃyojya bhāṣante bahudhā mānagarvitāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.2.97">
                        <l> bahu tadbhāṣitaṃ teṣāṃ ṣaṭsveṣvevāvatiṣṭhate |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśeṣā iva sāmānye ṣaṭtvaṃ tu paramaṃ matam||</l>
                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.5">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.43ab">
                        <l> jīrṇasya tu manuṣyasya varjayec calitān dvijān | </l>
                    </lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.43cd">
                        <l> nāsāṃ sannāṃ vivṛttāṃ vā ṛjvīṃ kṛtvā śalākayā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.44">
                        <l> pṛthaṅnasikayor nāḍyau dvimukhyau saṃpraveśayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             tataḥ paṭṭena saṃveṣṭya ghṛtasekaṃ pradāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.45">
                        <l> bhagnaṃ karṇaṃ tu badhnīyāt samaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaplutam |
                            <caesura/>
                             sadyaḥkṣatavidhānaṃ ca tataḥ pañcāt samācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.46">
                        <l> mastuluṅgād vinā bhinne kapāle madhusarpiṣī |
                            <caesura/>
                             dattvā tato nibadhnīyāt saptāhaṃ ca pibed ghṛtam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.47">
                        <l> patanād abhighātād vā śūnam aṅgaṃ yad akṣatam |
                            <caesura/>
                             śītān pradehān sekāṃś ca bhiṣak tasyāvacārayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="502" n="418b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.48">
                        <l> atha jaṅghorubhagnānāṃ kapāṭaśayanaṃ hitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kīlakā bandhanārthaṃ ca pañca kāryā vijānatā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.49">
                        <l> yathā na calanaṃ tasya bhagnasya kriyate tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             sandher ubhayato dvau dvau tale caikaś ca kīlakaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.50">
                        <l> śroṇyāṃ vā pṛṣṭhavaṃśe vā vakṣasy akṣakayos tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhagnasandhivimokṣeṣu vidhim enaṃ samācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.51">
                        <l> sandhīṃś ciravimuktāṃs tu snigdhān svinnān mṛdūkṛtān |
                            <caesura/>
                             uktair vidhānair buddhyā ca samyak prakṛtimānayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.52">
                        <l> kāṇḍabhagne prarūḍhe tu viṣamolbaṇasaṃhite |
                            <caesura/>
                             āpothya samayed bhagnaṃ tato bhagnavadācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.53">
                        <l> kalpayen nirgataṃ śuṣkaṃ vraṇānte 'sthi samāhitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sandhyante vā kriyāṃ kuryāt savraṇe vraṇabhagnavat || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="503" n="419a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.54">
                        <l> ūrdhvakāye tu bhagnānāṃ mastiṣkyaṃ karṇapūraṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             ghṛtapānaṃ hitaṃ nasyaṃ praśākhāsv anuvāsanam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.55">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi tailaṃ bhagnaprasādhakam |
                            <caesura/>
                             rātrau rātrau tilān kṛṣṇān vāsayed asthire jale || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.56">
                        <l> divā divā śoṣayitvā gavāṃ kṣīreṇa bhāvayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             tṛtīyaṃ saptarātraṃ tu bhāvayenmadhukāmbunā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.57">
                        <l> tataḥ kṣīraṃ punaḥ pītān suśuṣkāṃś cūrṇayed bhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             kākolyādiṃ sayaṣṭyāhvaṃ mañjiṣṭhāṃ sārivāṃ tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.58">
                        <l> kuṣṭhaṃ sarjarasaṃ māṃsīṃ suradāru sacandanam |
                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.59">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.60">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.61">
                        <l> saireyakaṃ kṣīraśuklāmanantāṃ samadhūlikām |
                            <caesura/>
                             piṣṭvā śṛṅgāṭakaṃ caiva pūrvoktāny auṣadhāni ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.62">
                        <l> ebhis tadvipacettailaṃ śāstravinmṛdunā 'gninā |
                            <caesura/>
                             etat tailaṃ sadā pathyaṃ bhagnānāṃ sarvakarmasu || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.63">
                        <l> ākṣepake pakṣaghāte tāluśoṣe tathā 'rdite |
                            <caesura/>
                             manyāstambhe śiroroge karṇaśūle hanugrahe || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.64">
                        <l> bādhirye timire caiva ye ca strīṣu kṣayaṃ gatāḥ |
                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.65">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.66">

                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.67">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.68">

                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.69">

                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.3.70">

                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.5">
                        <l> pakvāśayagate cāpi deyaṃ snehavirecanam |

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.6">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.7">

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="506" n="422b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.27">
                        <l> trivṛddantīsuvarṇakṣīrīsaptalāśaṅkhinītriphalāviḍaṅgānām akṣasamāḥ
                            bhāgāḥ, bilvamātraḥ kalkastilvakamūlakampillakayoḥ,
                            triphalārasadadhipātre dve dve, ghṛtapātram ekaṃ, tadaikadhyaṃ saṃsṛjya
                            vipacet; tilvakasarpir etat snehavirecanam upadiśanti vātarogiṣu |
                            tilvakavidhir evāśokaramyakayor draṣṭavyaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.28">
                        <l> tilaparipīḍanopakaraṇakāṣṭhānyāhṛtyānalpakālaṃ tailaparipītāny aṇūni
                            khaṇḍaśaḥ kalpayitvā'vakṣudya mahati kaṭāhe pānīyenābhiplāvya kvāthayet,
                            tataḥ sneham ambupṛṣṭhād yad udeti tat sarakapāṇyor anyatareṇādāya
                                vātaghnauṣadha<pc> </pc>pratīvāpaṃ snehapākakalpena vipacet, etad
                            aṇutailam upadiśanti vātarogiṣu; aṇubhyas tailadravyebhyo niṣpādyata ity
                            aṇutailam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="507" n="423a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.29">
                        <l> atha mahāpañcamūlakāṣṭhair bahubhir avadahyāvanipradeśam asitam uṣitam
                            ekarātram upaśānte 'gnāv apohya bhasma nivṛttāṃ bhūmiṃ
                            vidārigandhādisiddhena tailaghaṭaśatena tulyapayasā 'bhiṣicyaikarātram
                            avasthāpya tato yāvatī mṛttikā snigdhā syāt tām ādāyoṣṇodakena mahati
                            kaṭāhe 'bhyāsiñcet, tatra yat tailam uttiṣṭhet tat pāṇibhyāṃ paryādāya
                            svanuguptaṃ nidadhyāt; tatas tailaṃ
                            vātaharauṣadhakvāthamāṃsarasakṣīrāmlabhāgasahasreṇa sahasrapākaṃ vipaced
                            yāvatā kālena śaknuyāt paktuṃ, prativāpaś cātra haimavatā
                            dakṣiṇāpathagāś ca gandhā vātaghnāni ca, tasmin sidhyati śaṅkhān
                            ādhmāpayed dundubhīn āghātayec chatraṃ dhārayed bālavyajanaiś ca vījayed
                            brāhmaṇasahasraṃ bhojayet, tat sādhu siddham avatārya sauvarṇe rājate
                            mṛnmaye vā pātre svanuguptaṃ nidadhyāt, tad etat sahasrapākam
                            aprativāravīryaṃ rājārhaṃ tailam; evaṃ bhāgaśata<pc> </pc>vipakvaṃ
                            śatapākam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.30">
                        <l> gandharvahasta<pc> </pc>muṣkaka<pc> </pc>naktamālāṭa<pc
                                > </pc>rūṣaka<pc> </pc>pūtīkāragvadha<pc> </pc>citrakādīnāṃ patrāṇy
                            ārdrāṇi lavaṇena sahodūkhale 'vakṣudya snehaghaṭe prakṣipyāvalipya
                            gośakṛdbhir dāhayet; etat patralavaṇam upadiśanti vātarogeṣu || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.31">
                        <l> evaṃ snuhīkāṇḍavārtākuśigrulavaṇāni saṃkṣudya ghaṭaṃ pūrayitvā
                            sarpistailavasāmajjabhiḥ prakṣipyāvalipya gośakṛdbhir dāhayet; etat
                            snehalavaṇam upadiśanti vātarogeṣu |(iti kāṇḍalavaṇam) || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.4.32">
                        <l> gaṇḍīra<pc> </pc>palāśa<pc> </pc>kuṭaja<pc> </pc>bilvārka<pc
                                > </pc>snuhy<pc> </pc>apāmārga<pc> </pc>pāṭalā<pc
                                > </pc>pāribhadraka<pc> </pc>nādeyī<pc> </pc>kṛṣṇagandhā<pc
                                > </pc>nīpa<pc> </pc>nimba<pc> </pc>nirdahany<pc
                                > </pc>aṭarūṣaka<pc> </pc>naktamālaka<pc> </pc>pūtika<pc
                                > </pc>bṛhatī<pc> </pc>kaṇṭakārikā<pc
                                > </pc>bhallātakeṅgudī<pc> </pc>vaijayantī<pc> </pc>kadalī<pc
                                > </pc>bāṣpadvayekṣurakendravāruṇī<pc> </pc>śvetamokṣakāśokā ity
                            evaṃ vargaṃ samūlapatraśākham ārdram āhṛtya lavaṇena saha saṃsṛjya
                            pūrvavad dagdhvā kṣārakalpena parisrāvya vipacet, prativāpaś cātra
                            hiṅgvādibhiḥ pippalyādibhir vā | ity etat kalyāṇakalavaṇaṃ
                            vātarogagulmaplīhāgniṣaṅgājīrṇārśo 'rocakārtānāṃ kāsādibhiḥ kṛmibhir
                            upadrutānāṃ copadiśanti pānabhojaneṣv apīti || </l>

                        <l> bhavati cātra
                            <caesura/>
                             viṣyandanāduṣṇabhāvād doṣāṇāṃ ca vipācanāt |

                <div n="5" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.1">
                        <l> athāto mahāvātavyādhicikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.3">
                        <l> dvividhaṃ vātaśoṇitam uttānam avagāḍhaṃ cety eke bhāṣante; tat tu na
                            samyak, tad dhi kuṣṭhavad uttānaṃ bhūtvā kālāntareṇāvagāḍhībhavati,
                            tasmān na dvividham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.4">
                        <l> tatra balavadvigrahādibhiḥ prakupitasya vāyor gurūṣṇādhyaśanaśīlasya

                            vātaraktanimittāṃ vedanāṃ janayatīti vātaraktam | tat tu pūrvaṃ
                            hastapādayor avasthānaṃ kṛtvā paścād dehaṃ vyāpnoti | tasya pūrvarūpāṇi
                                toda<pc> </pc>dāha<pc> </pc>kaṇḍū<pc> </pc>śopha<pc
                                > </pc>stambha<pc> </pc>tvakpāruṣya<pc> </pc>sirā<pc
                                > </pc>snāyu<pc> </pc>dhamanī<pc> </pc>spandana<pc
                                > </pc>sakthi<pc> </pc>daurbalyāni śyāvāruṇamaṇḍalotpattiś cākasmāt
                            pāṇipādatalāṅguligulphamaṇibandhaprabhṛtiṣu, tatrāpratikāriṇo 'pacāriṇaś
                            ca rogo vyaktataraḥ, tasya lakṣaṇam uktaṃ; tatrāpratikāriṇo vaikalyaṃ
                            bhavati || </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.39">
                        <l> yadā syātāṃ parikṣīṇe bhūyiṣṭhe kaphamedasī |
                            <caesura/>
                             tadā snehādikaṃ karma punaratrāvacārayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.40">
                        <l> sugandhiḥ sulaghuḥ sūkṣmastīkṣṇoṣṇaḥ kaṭuko rase |
                            <caesura/>
                             kaṭupākaḥ saro hṛdyo gugguluḥ snigdhapicchilaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.41">
                        <l> sa navo bṛṃhaṇo vṛṣyaḥ purāṇastvapakarṣaṇaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             taikṣṇyauṣṇyātkaphavātaghnaḥ saratvānmalapittanut || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.42">
                        <l> saugandhyāt pūtikoṣṭhaghnaḥ saukṣmyāccānaladīpanaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             taṃ prātastriphalādārvīpaṭolakuśavāribhiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.43">
                        <l> pibed āvāpya vā mūtraiḥ kṣārair uṣṇodakena vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             jīrṇe yūṣarasaiḥ kṣīrair bhuñjāno hanti māsataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.44">
                        <l> gulmaṃ mehamudāvartamudaraṃ sabhagandaram |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṛmikṇḍvaruciśvitrāṇyarbudaṃ granthim eva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.45">
                        <l> nāḍyāḍhyavātaśvayathūn kuṣṭhaduṣṭavraṇāṃś ca saḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             koṣṭhasandhyasthigaṃ vāyuṃ vṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.5.trailer">
                        <l>iti śrīsuśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne mahāvātavyādhicikitsitaṃ nāma
                            pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||5 ||</l>

                <div n="6" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.1">
                        <l> athāto 'rśasāṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.3">
                        <l> caturvidho 'rśasāṃ sādhanopāyaḥ | tad yathā bheṣajaṃ kṣāro 'gniḥ śastram
                            iti | tatra acirakālajātānyalpadoṣaliṅgopadravāṇi bheṣajasādhyāni,
                            mṛduprasṛtāvagāḍhānyucchritāni kṣāreṇa,
                            karkaśasthirapṛthukaṭhinānyagninā, tanumūlanyucchritāni kledavanti ca
                            śastreṇa | tatra bheṣajasādhyānāmarśasāmadṛśyānāṃ ca bheṣajaṃ bhavati,
                            kṣārāgniśastrasādhyānāṃ tu vidhānamucyamānam upadhāraya || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.4">
                        <l> tatra balavantamāturamarśobhir upadrutam upasnigdhaṃ parisvinnam
                            anilavedanābhivṛddhipraśamārthaṃ snigdham uṣṇam alpam annaṃ dravaprāyaṃ
                            bhuktavantam upaveśya saṃvṛ(bhṛ)te śucau deśe sādhāraṇe vyabhre kāle
                            same phalake śayyāyāṃ vā pratyādity agudam anyasyotsaṅge
                            niṣaṇṇapūrvakāyam uttānaṃ kiñcid unnatakaṭikaṃ vastrakambalakopaviṣṭaṃ
                            yantraṇaśāṭakena parikṣiptagrīvāsakthiṃ parikarmibhiḥ suparigṛhītam
                            aspandanaśarīraṃ kṛtvā tato 'smai ghṛtābhyaktagudāya ghṛtābhyaktaṃ
                            yantramṛjvanumukhaṃ pāyau śanaiḥ śanaiḥ pravāhamāṇasya praṇidhāya,
                            praviṣṭe cārśo vīkṣya, śalākayotpīḍya, picuvastrayor anyatareṇa
                            pramṛjya, kṣāraṃ pātayet; pātayitvā ca pāṇinā yantradvāraṃ pidhāya
                            vākcchatamātramupekṣeta, tataḥ parmṛjya kṣārabalaṃ vyādhibalaṃ cāvekṣya
                            punarālepayet, athārśaḥ pakvajāmbavapratīkāśamavasannamīṣannatam abhis
                            amīkṣyopāvartayet, kṣāraṃ prakṣālayed dhānyāmlena
                            dadhimastuśuktaphalāmlair vā, tato yaṣṭīmadhukamiśreṇa sarpiṣā nirvāpya
                            yantramapanīyotthāpyāturamuṣṇodakopaviṣṭaṃ śītābhir adbhiḥ pariṣiñcet,
                            aśītābhir ity eke, tato nirvātamāgāraṃ praveśyācārikam ādiśet; sāvaśeṣaṃ
                            punardahet; evaṃ saptarātrāt saptarātrādekaikam upakrameta; tatra bahuṣu
                            pūrvṃa dakṣiṇaṃ sādhayet, dakṣiṇādvāmaṃ, vāmāt pṛṣṭhajaṃ, tato 'grajam
                            iti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="515" n="431a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.5">
                        <l> tatra vātaśleṣmanimittānyagnikṣārābhyāṃ sādhayet, kṣāreṇaiva mṛdunā
                            pittaraktanimittāni || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.6">
                        <l> tatra vātānulomyamannaruciragnidīptirlāghavaṃ
                            balavarṇotpattirmanastuṣṭiriti samyagdagdhaliṅgāni, atidagdhe tu
                            gudāvadaraṇaṃ dāho mūrcchā jvaraḥ pipāsā śoṇitātipravṛttistannimittāś
                            copadravā bhavanti, dhyāmālpavraṇatā
                            kaṇḍūranilavaiguṇyamindiryāṇāmaprasādo vikārasya cāśāntirhīnadagdhe ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.7">
                        <l> mahānti ca prāṇavatacchittvā dahet, nirgatāni cātyarthaṃ doṣapūrṇāni
                            yantrādvinā svedābhyaṅgasnehāvagāhopanāhavisrāvaṇālepakṣārāgniśastrair
                            upācaret; pravṛttaraktāni ca raktapittavidhānena, bhinnapurīṣāṇi
                            cātīsāravidhānena, baddhavarcāṃsi snehapānavidhānenodāvartavidhānena vā;
                            eṣa sarvasthānagatānāmarśasāṃ dahanakalpaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.8">
                        <l> āsādya ca darvīkūrcakaśalākānām anyatamena kṣāraṃ pātayet |
                            bhraṣṭagudasya tu vinā yantreṇa kṣārādikarma prayuñjīta | sarveṣu ca
                            śāliṣaṣṭikayavagodhūmānnaṃ sarpiḥsnigdham upaseveta payasā nimbayūṣeṇa
                            paṭolayūṣeṇa vā, yathādoṣaṃ śākair
                            vāstūkataṇḍulīyakajīvantyupodikāśvabalābalamūlakapālaṅkyasanacillīcuccūkalāyavallībhir
                            anyair vā | yaccānyadapi snigdhamagnidīpanamarśoghnaṃ sṛṣṭamūtrapurīṣaṃ
                            ca tadupaseveta || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.9">
                        <l> dagdheṣu cārśaḥsvabhyakto
                            'nalasandhukṣaṇārthamanilaprakopasaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ ca snehādīnāṃ
                            sāmānyataḥ kriyāpatham upaseveta | viśeṣatas tu vātārśaḥsu sarpīṣi ca
                            vātaharadīpanīyasiddhāni hiṅgvādibhiś cūrṇaiḥ pratisaṃsṛjya pibet,
                            pittārśaḥsu pṛthakparṇyādīnāṃ kaṣāyeṇa dīpanīyapratīvāpaṃ sarpiḥ,
                            śoṇitārśaḥsumañjiṣṭhāmuruṅgyādīnāṃ kaṣāye pācayet, śleṣmārśaḥsu
                            surasādīnāṃ kaṣāye | upadravāṃś ca yathāsvamupācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.10">
                        <l>
                            <pb facs="516" n="432a"/> paraṃ ca yatnamāsthāya gude
                            kṣārāgniśastrāṇyavacārayet, tadvibhramāddhi
                            ṣāṇḍhyaśophadāhamadamūrchāṭopānāhātīsārapravāhaṇāni bhavanti maraṇaṃ vā
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.11">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvaṃ yantrapramāṇam upadekṣyāmaḥ tatra yantraṃ lauhaṃ dāntaṃ
                            śārṅgaṃ vārkṣaṃ vā gostanākāraṃ caturaṅgulāyataṃ pañcāṅgulapariṇāhaṃ
                            puṃsāṃ, ṣaḍaṅgulapariṇāhaṃ nārīṇāṃ talāyataṃ; tad dvicchidraṃ
                            darśanārthaṃ, ekacchidraṃ tu karmaṇi; ekadvāre hi
                            śastrakṣārāgnīnāmatikramo na bhavati; chidrapramāṇaṃ tu
                            tryaṅgulāyatamaṅguṣṭhodarapariṇāhaṃ, yadaṅgulamavaśiṣṭaṃ
                            tasyārdhāṅgulādadhastādardhāṅgulocchritoparivṛttakarṇikam; eṣa
                            yantrākṛtisamāsaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.12">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvamarśasāmālepān vakṣyāmaḥ snuhīkṣīrayuktaṃ haridrācūrṇam ālepaḥ
                            prathamaḥ, kukkuṭapurīṣaguñjāharidrāpippalīcūrṇam iti gomūtrapittapiṣṭo
                            dvitīyaḥ, dantīcitrakasuvarcikālāṅgalīkalko vā gopittapiṣṭastṛtīyaḥ,
                            pippalīsaindhavakuṣṭhaśirīṣaphalakalkaḥ snuhīkṣīrapiṣṭo 'rkakṣīrapiṣṭo
                            vā caturthaḥ,
                            kāsīsaharitālasaindhavāśvamārakaviḍaṅgapūtīkakṛtavedhanajambvarkottamāraṇīdantīcitrakālarkasnuhīpayaḥsu
                            tailaṃ vipakvamabhyañjanenārśaḥ śātayati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.13">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvaṃ bheṣajasādhyeṣv adṛśyeṣv arśaḥsu yogān yāpanārthaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ
                            prātaḥ pratarguḍaharītakīmāseveta, brahmacārī gomūtradroṇasiddhaṃ vā
                            harītakīśataṃ prātaḥ prātar yathābalaṃ kṣaudreṇa, apāmārgamūlaṃ vā
                            taṇḍulodakena sakṣaudram aharahaḥ, śatāvarīmūlakalkaṃ vā kṣīreṇa,
                            citrakacūrṇayuktaṃ vā sīdhuṃ parārdhyaṃ bhallātacūrṇayuktaṃ vā
                            saktumanthamalavaṇaṃ takreṇa, kalaśe vā 'ntaś citrakamūlakalkāvalipte
                            niṣiktaṃ takramamlamanamlaṃ vā pānabhojaneṣūpayuñjīta, eṣa eva
                            bhārgyāsphotāyavānyāmalakaguḍūcīṣu takrakalpaḥ,
                            pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakaviḍaṅgaśuṇṭhīharītakīṣu ca pūrvavadeva
                            niranno vā takram aharahar māsamupaseveta,
                            śṛṅgaverapunarnavācitrakakaṣāyasiddhaṃ vā payaḥ, kṛṭajamūlatvakphāṇitaṃ
                            vā pippalyādipratīvāpaṃ kṣaudreṇa, mahāvātavyādhyuktaṃ hiṅgvādicūrṇam
                            upaseveta takrāhāraḥ kṣīrāhāro vā, kṣāralavaṇāṃś
                            citrakamūlakṣārodakasiddhaṃ vā payaḥ palāśatarukṣārasiddhaṃ vā,
                            palāśatarukṣārasiddhān vā kulmāṣān, pāṭalāpāmārgabṛṃhatīpalāśakṣāraṃ vā
                            parisrutam aharahar ghṛtasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ, kuṭajabandākamūlakalkaṃ vā takreṇa,
                            citrakapūtīkanāgarakalkaṃ vā pūtīkakṣāreṇa, kṣārodakasiddhaṃ vā sarpiḥ
                            pippalyādipratīvāpaṃ, kṛṣṇatilaprasṛtaṃ prakuñcaṃ vā prātaḥ prātar
                            upaseveta śītodakānupānam; ebhir abhivardhate 'gnirarśāṃsi copaśāmyanti
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="517" n="433b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.14">
                        <l> dvipañcamūlīdantīcitrakapathyānāṃ tulāmāhṛtya jalacaturdroṇe vipācayet ,
                            tataḥ pādāvaśiṣṭaṃ kaṣāyam ādāya suśītaṃ guḍatulayā sahonmiśrya
                            ghṛtabhājane niḥkṣipya māsamupekṣeta yavapalle, tataḥ prātaḥ prātar
                            mātrāṃ pāyayeta, tenārśograhaṇīdoṣapāṇḍurogodāvartārocakā na bhavanti
                            dīptaś cāgnir bhavati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.15">
                        <l> pippalīmaricaviḍaṅgailavālukalodhrāṇāṃ dve dve pale, indravāruṇyāḥ pañca
                            palāni, kapitthamadhyasya daśa, pathyāphalānāmardhaprasthaḥ, prastho
                            dhātrīphalānāṃ, etadaikadhyaṃ jalacaturdroṇe vipācya, pādāvaśeṣaṃ
                            parisrāvya, suśītaṃ guḍatulādvayenonmiśrya, ghṛtabhājane niḥkṣipya,
                            pakṣamupekṣeta yavapalle; tataḥ prātaḥ prātar yathābalam upayuñjīta |
                            eṣa khalvariṣṭaḥ
                            plīhāgniṣaṅgārśograhaṇīhṛtpāṇḍurogaśophakuṣṭhagulmodarakṛmiharo
                            balavarṇakaraś ceti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.16">
                        <l> tatra vātaprāyeṣu
                            snehasvedavamanavirecanāsthāpanānuvāsanamapratisiddhaṃ, pittajeṣu
                            virecanam, evaṃ raktajeṣu saṃśamanaṃ, kaphajeṣu
                            śṛṅgaverakulatthopayogaḥ, sarvadoṣaharaṃ yathoktaṃ sarvajeṣu,
                            yathāsvauṣadhisiddhaṃ ca payaḥ sarveṣviti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.17">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvaṃ bhallātakavidhānam upadekṣyāmaḥ bhallātakāni
                            paripakvānyanupahatānyāhṛtya tata ekamādāya dvidhā tridhā caturdhā vā
                            chedayitvā kaṣāyakalpena vipācya tasya kaṣāyasya śuktimanuṣṇāṃ
                            ghṛtābhyaktatālujihvauṣṭhaḥ prātaḥ prātar upaseveta, tato 'parāhṇe
                            kṣīraṃ sarpirodana ity āhāraḥ; evam ekaikaṃ vardhayed yāvat <pb
                                facs="518" n="434a"/>pañceti, tataḥ pañca pañcābhivardhayed yāvat
                            saptatiriti, prāpya ca saptatimapakarṣayed bhūyaḥ pañca pañca yāvat
                            pañceti, pañcabhyastvekaikaṃ yāvadekam iti | evaṃ bhallātakasahasrām
                            upayujya sarvakuṣṭhārśobhir vimukto balavānarogaḥ śatāyur bhavati ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.18">
                        <l> dvivraṇīyoktena vidhānena bhallātakaniś cyutitaṃ snehamādāya prātaḥ
                            prātaḥ śuktimātram upayuñjīta, jīrṇe pūrvavadāhāraḥ phalaprakarṣaś ca |
                            bhallātakamajjabhyo vā snehamādāyāpakṛṣṭadoṣaḥ pratisaṃsṛṣṭabhakto
                            nivātamāgāraṃ praviśya yathābalaṃ prasṛtiṃ prakuñcaṃ vopayuñjīta,
                            tasmiñjīrṇe kṣīraṃ sarpirodana ity āhāraḥ, evaṃ māsamupayujya
                            māsatrayamādiṣṭāhāro rakṣedātmānaṃ; tataḥ sarvopatāpānapahṛtya varṇavān
                            balavāñ śravaṇagrahaṇadhāraṇaśaktisaṃpanno varṣaśatāyurbhavati, māse
                            māse ca prayoge varṣaśataṃ varṣaśatamāyuṣo 'bhivṛddhirbhavati, evaṃ
                            daśamāsānupayujya varṣasahasrāyur bhavati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.19">
                        <l> bhavanti cātra yathā sarvāṇi kuṣṭhāni hataḥ svadirabījakau |
                            <caesura/>
                             tathaivārśāṃsi sarvāṇi vṛkṣakāruṣkarau hataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.20">
                        <l> haridrāyāḥ prayogeṇa pramehā iva ṣoḍaśa |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣārāgnī nātivartante tathā dṛśyā gudodbhavāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.21">
                        <l> ghṛtāni dīpanīyāni lehāyaskṛtayaḥ surāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             āsavāś ca prayoktavyā vīkṣya doṣasamucchritam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.22">
                        <l> vegāvarodhastrīpṛṣṭhayānānyutkuṭukāsanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             yathāsvaṃ doṣalaṃ cānnamarśaḥsu parivarjayet ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne 'rśaś cikitsitaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ
