                <div n="13" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <pb facs="540" n="456a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.1">
                        <l> athāto madhumehacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.3">
                        <l> madhumehitvamāpannaṃ bhiṣagbhiḥ parivarjitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             yogenānena matimān pramehiṇamupācaret || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.4">
                        <l> māse śukre śucau caiva śailāḥ sūryāṃśutāpitāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             jatuprakāśaṃ svarasaṃ śilābhyaḥ prasravanti hi || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.5">
                        <l> śilājatviti vikhyātaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             trapvādīnāṃ tu lohānāṃ ṣaṇṇāmanyatamānvayam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.6">
                        <l> jñeyaṃ svagandhataś cāpi ṣaḍyoni prathitaṃ kṣitau |
                            <caesura/>
                             lohādbhavati tadyasmācchilājatu jatuprabham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.7">
                        <l> tasya lohasya tadvīryaṃ rasaṃ cāpi bibharti tat |
                            <caesura/>
                             trapusīsāyasādīni pradhānānyuttarottaram || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.8">
                        <l> yathā tathā prayoge 'pi śreṣṭhe śreṣṭhaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tat sarvaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ kaṣāyānurasaṃ saram || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.9">
                        <l> kaṭupākyuṣṇavīryaṃ ca śoṣaṇaṃ chedanaṃ tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             teṣu yat kṛṣṇamalaghu snigdhaṃ niḥśarkaraṃ ca yat || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.10">
                        <l> gomūtragandhi yac cāpi tat pradhānaṃ pracakṣate |
                            <caesura/>
                             tadbhāvitaṃ sāragaṇair hṛtadoṣo dinodaye || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.11">
                        <l> pibet sārodakenaiva ślakṣṇapiṣṭaṃ yathābalam |
                            <caesura/>
                             jāṅgalena rasenānnaṃ tasmiñjīrṇe tu bhojayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.12">
                        <l> upayujya tulām evaṃ girijād amṛtopamāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             vapur varṇabalopeto madhumehavivarjitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.13ab">
                        <l> jīved varṣaśataṃ pūrṇamajaro 'marasannibhaḥ | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.13cd">
                        <l> śataṃ śataṃ tulāyāṃ tu sahasraṃ daśataulike || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.14">
                        <l> bhallātakavidhānena parihāravidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             mehaṃ kuṣṭham apasmāram unmādaṃ ślīpadaṃ garam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.15ab">
                        <l> śoṣaṃ śophārśasī gulmaṃ pāṇḍutāṃ viṣamajvaram | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.15cd">
                        <l> apohaty acirāt kālāc chilājatu niṣevitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.16">
                        <l> na so 'sti rogo yaṃ cāpi nihanyānna śilājatu |
                            <caesura/>
                             śarkarāṃ cirasaṃbhūtāṃ bhinatti ca tathā 'śmarīm || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.17">
                        <l> bhāvanāloḍane cāsya kartavye bheṣajair hitaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             evaṃ ca mākṣikaṃ dhātuṃ tāpījam amṛtopamam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.18">
                        <l> madhuraṃ kāñcanābhāsam amlaṃ vā rajataprabham |
                            <caesura/>
                             piban hanti jarākuṣṭhamehapāṇḍvāmayakṣayān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.19">
                        <l> tadbhāvitaḥ kapotāṃś ca kulatthāṃś ca vivarjayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             pañcakarmaguṇātītaṃ śraddhāvantaṃ jijīviṣum || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.20">
                        <l> yogenānena matimān sādhayed api kuṣṭhanam |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="541" n="457a"/> vṛkṣās tuvarakā ye syuḥ paścimārṇava<pc
                            > </pc>bhūmiṣu || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.21ab">
                        <l> vīcītaraṅgavikṣepamārutoddhūtapallavāḥ | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.21cd">
                        <l> teṣāṃ phalāni gṛhṇīyāt supakvānyambudāgame || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.22">
                        <l> majjāṃ tebhyo 'pi saṃhṛtya śoṣayitvā vicūrṇya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             tilavat pīḍayed droṇyāṃ srāvayed vā kusumbhavat || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.23">
                        <l> tattailaṃ saṃhṛtaṃ bhūyaḥ pacedātoyasaṃkṣayāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             avatārya karīṣe ca pakṣamātraṃ nidhāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.24">
                        <l> snigdhaḥ svinno hṛtamalaḥ pakṣādūrdhvaṃ prayatnavān |
                            <caesura/>
                             caturthabhaktāntaritaḥ śuklādau divase śubhe || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.25">
                        <l> mantrapūtasya tailasya pibenmātrāṃ yathābalam |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatra mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi yenedam abhimantryate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.26">
                        <l> majjasāra mahāvīrya sarvān dhātūn viśodhaya |
                            <caesura/>
                             śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇistvāmājñāpayate 'cyutaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.27">
                        <l> tenāsyor dhvamadhaś cāpi doṣā yāntyasakṛttataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             asnehalavaṇāṃ sāyaṃ yavāgūṃ śītalāṃ pibet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.28">
                        <l> pañcāhaṃ prapibettailamanena vidhinā naraḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             pakṣaṃ pariharec cāpi mudgayūṣaudanāśanaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.29">
                        <l> pañcabhir divasair evaṃ sarvakuṣṭhair vimucyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             tad eva khadirakvāthe triguṇe sādhu sādhitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.30">
                        <l> nihitaṃ pūrvavat pakṣāt pibenmāsamatandritaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tenābhyaktaśarīraś ca kurvītāhāramīritam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.31">
                        <l> bhinnasvaraṃ raktanetraṃ viśīrṇaṃ kṛmibhakṣitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             anenāśu prayogeṇa sādhayet kuṣṭhinaṃ naram || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.32">
                        <l> sarpirmadhuyutaṃ pītaṃ tad eva khadirāmbunā |
                            <caesura/>
                             pakṣimāṃsarasāhāraṃ karoti dviśatāyuṣam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.33">
                        <l> tad eva nasye pañcāśaddivasānupayojitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             vapuṣmantaṃ śrutidharaṃ karoti triśatāyuṣam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.34ab">
                        <l> śodhayanti naraṃ pītā majjānas tasya mātrayā | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.34cd">
                        <l> mahāvīryastuvarakaḥ kuṣṭhamehāpahaḥ paraḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.35">
                        <l> sāntardhūmas tasya majjā tu dagdhaḥ kṣiptastaile saindhavaṃ cāñjanaṃ ca
                            |
                            <caesura/>
                             paillyaṃ hanyādarmanaktāndhyakācān nīlīrogaṃ taimiraṃ cāñjanena ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.13.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne madhumehacikitsitaṃ nāma trayodaśo
                            'dhyāyaḥ ||13 ||</l>
                    </lg>
                </div>

                <div n="14" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>caturdaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.1">
                        <l> athāta udarāṇāṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.3">
                        <l> aṣṭāv udarāṇi pūrvam uddiṣṭāni |
                            <caesura/>
                             teṣv asādhyaṃ baddhagudaṃ parisrāvi ca; avaśiṣṭāni kṛcchrasādhyāni; <pb
                                facs="542" n="458a"/>sarvāṇy eva pratyākhyāyopakrameta | teṣv ādyaś
                            caturvargo bheṣajasādhyaḥ, uttaraḥ śastrasādhyaḥ, kālaprakarṣāt
                            sarvāṇyeva śastrasādhyāni varjayitavyāni vā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.4">
                        <l> udarī tu gurvabhiṣyandirūkṣavidāhisnigdhapiśitapariṣekāvagāhān
                            pariharet; śāliṣaṣṭikayavagodhūmanīvārān nityam aśnīyāt || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.5">
                        <l> tatra vātodariṇaṃ vidārigandhādisiddhena sarpiṣā snehayitvā,
                            tilvakavipakvenānulomya, citrāphalatailapragāḍhena
                            vidārigandādikaṣāyeṇāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca, sālvaṇena copanāhayed
                            udaraṃ, bhojayec cainaṃ vidārigandhādisiddhena kṣīreṇa jāṅgalarasena ca,
                            svedayec cābhīkṣṇam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.6">
                        <l> pittodariṇaṃ tu madhuragaṇavipakvena sarpiṣā snehayitvā,
                            śyāmātriphalātrivṛdvipakvenānulomya, śarkarāmadhughṛtapragāḍhena
                            nyagrodhādikaṣāyeṇāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca, pāyasenopanāhayed udaraṃ,
                            bhojayec cainaṃ vidārigandhādisiddhena payasā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.7">
                        <l> śleṣmodariṇaṃ tu pippalyādikaṣāyasiddhena sarpiṣopasnehya,
                            snuhīkṣīravipakvenānulomya, trikaṭukamūtrakṣāratailapragāḍhena
                            muṣkakādikaṣāyeṇāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca,
                            śaṇātasīdhātakīkiṇvasarṣapamūlakabījakalkaiś copanāhayed udaraṃ,
                            bhojayec cainaṃ trikaṭukapragāḍhena kulatthayūṣeṇa pāyasena vā, svedayec
                            cābhīkṣṇam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.8">
                        <l> dūṣyodariṇaṃ tu pratyākhyāya saptalāśaṅkhinīsvarasasiddhena sarpiṣā
                            viracayen māsam ardhamāsaṃ vā, mahāvṛkṣakṣīrasurāgomūtrasiddhena vā;
                            śuddhakoṣṭhaṃ tu madyenāśvamārakaguñjākākādanīmūlakalkaṃ pāyayet;
                            ikṣukāṇḍāni vā kṛṣṇasarpeṇa daṃśayitvā bhakṣayed vallīphalāni vā,
                            mūlajaṃ kandajaṃ vā viṣamā sevayet, tenāgado bhavaty anyaṃ vā bhāvam
                            āpadyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.9">
                        <l> bhavati cātra kupitānilamūlatvāt saṃcitatvān malasya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarvodareṣu śaṃsanti bahuśas tv anulomanam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="543" n="459a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.10">
                        <l> ata ūrdhvaṃ sāmānyayogān vakṣyāmaḥ | tad yathā eraṇḍatailam aharahar
                            māsaṃ dvau vā kevalaṃ mūtrayuktaṃ kṣīrayuktaṃ vā sevetodakavarjī,
                            māhiṣaṃ vā mūtraṃ kṣīreṇa nirāhāraḥ saptarātram, uṣṭrīkṣīrāhāro vā
                            'nnavārivarjī pakṣaṃ, pippalīṃ vā māsaṃ pūrvoktena vidhānenāseveta,
                            saindhavājamodāyuktaṃ vā nikumbhatailam,
                            ārdraśṛṅgaverarasapātraśatasiddhaṃ vā vātaśūle 'vacāryaṃ,
                            śṛṅgaverarasavipakvaṃ kṣīram āseveta, cavyaśṛṅgaverakalkaṃ vā payasā
                            saraladevadārucitrakam eva vā, muraṅgīśālaparṇīśyāmāpunarnavākalkaṃ vā,
                            jyotiṣkaphalatailaṃ vā kṣīreṇa svarjikāhiṅgumiśraṃ pibet, <ref
                                rend="superscript                 footnote marker"
                            >*</ref>guḍadvitīyāṃ vā harītakīṃ bhakṣayet, srutīkṣīrabhāvitānāṃ vā
                            pippalīnāṃ sahasraṃ kālena, pathyākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ vā
                            snuhīkṣīrabhāvitamutkārikāṃ pakvāṃ dāpayet; harītakīcūrṇaṃ
                            prasthamāḍhake ghṛtasyāvāpyāṅgāreṣv abhivilāpya khajenābhimathyānuguptaṃ
                            kṛtvā 'rdhamāsaṃ yavapalle vāsayet, tataś coddhṛtya parisrāvya
                            harītakīkvāthāmladadhīnyāvāpya vipacet, tad yathā yogaṃ māsam ardhamāsaṃ
                            vā pāyayet; gavye payasi mahāvṛkṣakṣīramāvāpya vipacet, vipakvaṃ
                            cāvatārya śītībhūtaṃ manthānenābhimathya navanītamādāya bhūyo
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīreṇaiva vipacet, tad yathā yogaṃ māsaṃ māsārdhaṃ vā pāyayet;
                            cavyacitrakadantyativiṣākuṣṭhasārivātriphalājamodaharidrāśaṅkhinītrivṛttrikaṭukānām
                            ardhakārṣikā bhāgā, rājavṛkṣaphalamajjñāmaṣṭau karṣāḥ,
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīrapale dve, gavāṃ kṣīramūtrayor aṣṭāvaṣṭau palāni, etat
                            sarvaṃ ghṛtaprasthe samāvāpya vipacet, tad yathā yogaṃ māsam ardhamāsṃ
                            vā pāyayet; etāni tilvakaghṛtacaturthāni sarpīṣy
                            udaragulmavidradhyaṣṭhīlānāhakuṣṭhonmādāpasmāreṣūpayojyāni
                            virecanārthaṃ; mūtrāsavāriṣṭasurāś cābhīkṣṇaṃ mahāvṛkṣakṣīrasaṃbhṛtāḥ
                            seveta; virecanadravyakaṣāyaṃ vā śṛṅgaveradevadārupragāḍham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.11">
                        <l> vamanavirecanaśirovirecanadravyāṇāṃ pālikābhāgāḥ
                            pippalyādivacādiharidrādiparipaṭhitānāṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ ślakṣṇapiṣṭānāṃ
                            yathoktānāṃ ca lavaṇānāṃ tat sarvaṃ mūtragaṇe prakṣipya
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīraprasthaṃ ca mṛdvagninā 'vaghaṭṭayan vipaced
                            apradagdhakalkaṃ, tatsādhusiddham avatārya śītībhūtamakṣamātrā guṭikā
                            vartayet, tāsām ekāṃ dve tisro vā guṭikā balāpekṣayā māsāṃs trīṃś caturo
                            vā seveta, eṣā++<ref rend="superscript footnote marker"
                            >*</ref>ānāhavartikriyā viśeṣeṇa mahāvyādhiṣūpayujyate (viśeṣeṇa)
                            koṣṭhajāṃś ca kṛmīn apahanti
                            kāsaśvāsakṛmikuṣṭhapratiśyāyārocakāvipākodāvartāṃś ca nāśayati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="544" n="460a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.12">
                        <l>
                            madanaphalamajjakuṭajajīmūtakekṣvākudhāmārgavatrivṛttrikaṭukasarṣapalavaṇāni
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīramūtrayor anyatareṇa piṣṭvā 'ṅguṣṭhamātrāṃ vartiṃ
                            kṛtvodariṇa ānāhe tailalavaṇābhyaktagudasyaikāṃ dve tisro vā pāyau
                            nidadhyāt, eṣā ''ānāhavartikriyā vātamūtrapurīṣodāvartādhmānānāheṣu
                            vidheyā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.13">
                        <l> plīhodariṇaḥ snigdhasvinnasya dadhnā bhuktavato vāmabāhau
                            kūrparābhyantarataḥ sirāṃ vidhyet, vimardayec ca pāṇinā plīhānaṃ
                            rudhirasyandanārthaṃ; tataḥ saṃśuddhadehaṃ samudraśuktikākṣāraṃ payasā
                            pāyayeta, hiṅgusauvarcike vā kṣīreṇa, srutena palāśakṣāreṇa vā
                            yavakṣāraṃ, kiṃśukakṣārodakena vā bahuśaḥ srutena yavakṣāraṃ,
                            pārijātakekṣurakāpāmārgakṣāraṃ vā, tailasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ śobhāñjanakayūṣaṃ
                            pippalīsaindhavacitrakayuktaṃ, pūtikarañjakṣāraṃ vā 'mlasrutaṃ
                            viḍlavaṇapippalīprāgāḍham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.14">
                        <l> pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakaśṛṅgaverayavakṣārasaindhavānāṃ pālikā
                            bhāgāḥ, ghṛtaprasthaṃ tattulyaṃ ca kṣīraṃ tadaikadhyaṃ vipācayet , etat
                            ṣaṭpalakaṃ nāma sarpiḥ
                            plīhāgnisaṅgagulmodarodāvartaśvayathupāṇḍurogakāsaśvāsapratiśyāyor
                            dhvavātaviṣamajvarān apahanti | mandāgnirvā hiṅgvādikaṃ cūrṇam
                            upayuñjīta || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.15">
                        <l> yakṛddālye 'py eṣa eva kriyāvibhāgaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśeṣatas tu dakṣiṇabāhau sirāvyadhaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.16">
                        <l> maṇibandhaṃ sakṛnnāmya vāmāṅguṣṭhasamīritām |
                            <caesura/>
                             dahet sirāṃ śareṇāśu plīhno vaidyaḥ praśāntaye || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.17">
                        <l> baddhagude parisrāviṇi ca snigdhasvinnasyābhyaktasyādho nābher vāmataś
                            caturaṅgulam apahāya romarājyā udaraṃ pāṭayitvā
                            caturaṅgulapramāṇamantrāṇi niṣkṛṣya nirīkṣya
                            baddhagudasyāntrapratirodhakaramaśmānaṃ bālaṃ vā 'pohya malajātaṃ vā
                            tato madhusarpirbhyām abhyajyāntrāṇi yathāsthānaṃ sthāpayitvā <ref
                                rend="superscript               footnote marker">*</ref>bāhyaṃ
                            vraṇam udarasya sīvyet | parisrāviṇy apy evam eva
                            śalyamuddhṛtyāntrasrāvān saṃśodhya<ref rend="superscript footnote
                                marker">*</ref>, tacchidramāntraṃ samādhāya kālapipīlikābhir
                            daṃśayet, daṣṭe ca tāsāṃ kāyān apaharen na śirāṃsi, tataḥ pūrvavat
                            sīvyet, saṃdhānaṃ ca yathoktaṃ kārayet, yaṣṭīmadhukamiśrayā ca kṛṣṇamṛdā
                            'valipya bandhenopacaret, tato nivātamāgāraṃ praveśyācārikam upadiśet,
                            vāsayec cainaṃ tailadroṇyāṃ sarpirdroṇyāṃ vā payovṛttim iti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="545" n="461a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.18">
                        <l> dakodariṇas tu vātaharatailābhyaktasyoṣṇodakasvinnasya sthitasyāptaiḥ
                            suparigṛhītasyākakṣāt pariveṣṭitasyādho nābher vāmataś caturaṅgulam
                            apahāya romarājyā vrīhimukhenāṅguṣṭhodarapramāṇam avagāḍhaṃ vidhyet,
                            tatra trapvādīnām anyatamasya nāḍīṃ dvidvārāṃ pakṣanāḍīṃ vā saṃyojya
                            doṣodakam avasiñcet, toto nāḍīm apahṛtya tailalavaṇenābhyajya vraṇaṃ
                            bandhenopacaret, na caikasminn eva divase sarvaṃ doṣodakam apaharet,
                            sahasā hy apahṛte tṛṣṇājvarāṅgamardātīsāraśvāsakāsapādadāhā utpadyerann
                            āpūryate vā bhṛśataram udaram asañjātaprāṇasya, tasmāt
                            tṛtīyacaturthapañcamaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamadaśamadvādaśaṣoḍaśarātrāṇām anyatamam
                            antarīkṛtya doṣodakam alpālpam avasiñcet; niḥsṛte ca doṣe gāḍhataram
                            āvikakauśeyacarmaṇām anyatamena pariveṣṭayed udaraṃ, tathā nādhmāpayati
                            vāyuḥ; ṣaṇmāsāṃś ca payasā bhojayej jāṅgalarasena vā, tatas trīn māsān
                            ardhodakena payasā phalāmlena jāṅgalarasena vā, avaśiṣṭaṃ māsatrayam
                            annaṃ laghu hitaṃ vā seveta, evaṃ saṃvatsareṇāgado bhavati || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.19">
                        <l> bhavati cātra
                            <caesura/>
                             āsthāpane caiva virecane ca pāne tathā ''āhāravidhikriyāsu |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarvodaribhyaḥ kuśalaiḥ prayojyaṃ kṣīraṃ śṛtaṃ jāṅgalajo raso vā ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne udaracikitsitaṃ nāma caturdaśo
                            'dhyāyaḥ ||14 ||</l>
                    </lg>
                </div>

                <div n="15" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>pañcadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.1">
                        <l> athāto mūḍhagarbhacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.3">
                        <l> nāto 'nyat kaṣṭatamamasti yathā mūḍhagarbhaśalyoddharaṇam; atra hi
                            yoniyakṛtplīhāntravivaragarbhāśayānāṃ madhye karma kartavyaṃ sparśena,
                            utkarṣaṇāpakarṣaṇasthānāpavartanotkartanabhedanacchedanapīḍanarjūkaraṇadāraṇāni
                            caikahastena garbhaṃ garbhiṇīṃ cāhiṃsatā, tasmād adhipatimāpṛcchya paraṃ
                            ca yatnamāsthāyopakrameta || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.4">
                        <l> tatra samāsenāṣṭavidhā mūḍhagarbhagatiruddiṣṭā; svabhāvagatā api trayaḥ
                            saṅgā bhavanti śiraso vaiguṇyādaṃsayor jaghanasya vā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.5">
                        <l> jīvati tu garbhe sūtikāgarbhanirharaṇe prayateta |
                            <caesura/>
                             nirhartum aśakye cyāvanān mantrānupaśṛṇuyāt; tān vakṣyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="546" n="462a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.6">
                        <l> ihāmṛtaṃ ca somaś ca citrabhānuś ca bhāmini |
                            <caesura/>
                             uccaiḥśravāś ca turago mandire nivasantu te || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.7">
                        <l> idamamṛtamapāṃ samuddhṛtaṃ vai tava laghu garbhamimaṃ pramuñcatu stri |
                            <caesura/>
                             tad analapavanārkavāsavās te saha lavaṇāmbudharair diśantu śāntim ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.8">
                        <l> muktāḥ paśorvipāśāś ca muktāḥ sūryeṇa raśmayaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             muktaḥ sarvabhayād garbha ehyehi viramāvitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.9">
                        <l> auṣadhāni ca vidadhyādyathoktāni | mṛte cottānāyā ābhugnasakthyā
                            vastrādhārakonnamitakaṭhyā dhanvananagavṛttikāśālmalīmṛtsnaghṛtābhyāṃ
                            mrakṣayitvā hastaṃ yonau praveśya garbham upaharet | tatra sakthibhyām
                            āgatamanulomamevāñchet, ekasakthnā pratipannasyetarasakthi
                            prasāryāpaharet, sphigdeśenāgatasya sphigdeśaṃ prapīḍyordhvam utkṣipya
                            sakthinī prasāryāpaharet, tiryagāgatasya parighasyeva tiraścīnasya paś
                            cād ardhamūrdhvamutkṣipya pūrvārdhamapatyapathaṃ
                            pratyārjavamānīyāpaharet, pārśvāpavṛttaśirasamaṃsaṃ prapīḍyor
                            dhvamutkṣipya śiro 'patyapatham ānīyāpaharet, bāhudvayapratipannasyor
                            dhvamutpīḍyāṃsau śiro 'nulomamānīyāpaharet, dvāvantyāvasādhyau
                            mūḍhagarbhau, evamaśakye śastramavacārayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.10">
                        <l> sacetanaṃ ca śastreṇa na kathañcana dārayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             dāryamāṇo hi jananīmātmānaṃ caiva ghātayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.11">
                        <l> aviṣahye vikāre tu śreyo garbhasya pātanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             na garbhiṇyā viparyāsastasmāt prāptaṃ na hāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.12">
                        <l> tataḥ striyamāśvāsya maṇḍalāgreṇāṅgulīśastreṇa vā śiro vidārya,
                            śiraḥkapālānyāhṛtya, śaṅkunā gṛhītvorasi kakṣāyāṃ vā 'paharet;
                            abhinnaśirasamakṣikūṭe gaṇḍe vā, aṃsasaṃsaktasyāṃsadeśe bāhū chittvā,
                            dṛtimivātataṃ vātapūrṇodaraṃ vā vidārya nirasyāntrāṇi
                            śithilībhūtamāharet, jaghanasaktasya vā jaghanakapālānīti || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.13">
                        <l> kiṃbahunā yadyadaṅgaṃ hi garbhasya tasya sajjati tadbhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             samyagvinirharecchittvā rakṣennārīṃ ca yatnataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.14">
                        <l> garbhasya gatayaś citrā jāyante 'nilakopataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatrānalpamatirvaidyo varteta vidhipūrvakam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.15">
                        <l> nopekṣeta mṛtaṃ garbhaṃ muhūrtam api paṇḍitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sa hyāśu jananīṃ hanti nirucchvāsaṃ paśuṃ yathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.16">
                        <l> maṇḍalāgreṇa kartavyaṃ chedyam antarvijānatā |
                            <caesura/>
                             vṛddhipatraṃ hi tīkṣṇāgraṃ nārīṃ hiṃsyāt kadācana || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.17">
                        <l> athāpatantīm aparāṃ pātayet pūrvavad bhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             hastenāpahared vā 'pi pārśvabhyāṃ paripīḍya vā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="547" n="463a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.18">
                        <l> dhunuyāc ca muhurnārīṃ pīḍayed vā 'ṃsapiṇḍikām |
                            <caesura/>
                             tailāktayonerevaṃ tāṃ pātayenmatimān bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.19">
                        <l> evaṃ nirhṛtaśalyāṃ tu siñced uṣṇena vāriṇā |
                            <caesura/>
                             tato 'bhyaktaśarīrāyā yonau snehaṃ nidhāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.20">
                        <l> evaṃ mṛdvī bhaved yonistacchūlaṃ copaśāmyati |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṛṣṇātanmūlaśuṇṭhyelāhiṅgubhārgīḥ sadīpyakāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.21">
                        <l> vacāmativiṣāṃ rāsnāṃ cavyaṃ saṃcūrṇya pāyayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             snehena doṣasyandārthaṃ vedanopaśamāya ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.22">
                        <l> kvāthaṃ caiṣāṃ tathā kalkaṃ cūrṇaṃ vā snehavarjitam |
                            <caesura/>
                             śākatvagghiṅgvativiṣāpāṭhākaṭukarohiṇīḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.23">
                        <l> tathā tejovatīṃ cāpi pāyayet pūrvavadbhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             trirātraṃ pañcasaptāhaṃ tataḥ snehaṃ punaḥ pibet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.24">
                        <l> pāyayet āsavaṃ naktam ariṣṭaṃ vā susaṃskṛtam |
                            <caesura/>
                             śirīṣakakubhābhyāṃ ca toyam ācamane hitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.25">
                        <l> upadravāś ca ye 'nye syustān yathāsvamupācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarvataḥ pariśuddhā ca snigdhapathyālpabhojanā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.26">
                        <l> svedābhyaṅgaparā nityaṃ bhavet krodhavivarjitā |
                            <caesura/>
                             payo vātaharaiḥ siddhaṃ daśāhaṃ bhojane hitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.27">
                        <l> rasaṃ daśāhaṃ śeṣe tu yathāyogamupācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             vyupadravāṃ viśuddhāṃ ca jñātvā ca varavarṇinīm || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.28">
                        <l> ūrdhvaṃ caturbhyo māsebhyo visṛjet parihārataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="547" n="463b"/> yonisantarpaṇe 'bhyaṅge pāne bastiṣu bhojane
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.29">
                        <l> balātailamidaṃ cāsyai dadyādanilavāraṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             balāmūlakaṣāyasya daśamūlīśṛtasya ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.30">
                        <l> yavakolakulatthānāṃ kvāthasya payasas tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             aṣṭāvaṣṭau śubhā bhāgāstailādekastadekataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.31">
                        <l> pacedāvāpya madhuraṃ gaṇaṃ saindhavasaṃyutam |
                            <caesura/>
                             tathā 'guruṃ sarjarasaṃ saralaṃ devadāru ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.32">
                        <l> mañjiṣṭhāṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭhamelāṃ kālānusārivām |
                            <caesura/>
                             māṃsīṃ śaileyakaṃ patraṃ tagaraṃ sārivāṃ vacām || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.33">
                        <l> śatāvarīmaśvagandhāṃ śatapuṣpāṃ punarnavām |
                            <caesura/>
                             tat sādhusiddhaṃ sauvarṇe rājate mṛnmaye 'pi vā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.34">
                        <l> prakṣipya kalaśe samyak svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             balātailamidaṃ khyātaṃ sarvavātavikāranut || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.35">
                        <l> yathābalamato mātrāṃ sūtikāyai pradāpayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             yā ca garbhārthinī nārī kṣīṇaśukraś ca yaḥ pumān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.36">
                        <l> vātakṣīṇe marmahate mathite 'bhihate tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhagne śramābhipanne ca sarvathaivopayujyate || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.37">
                        <l> etad ākṣepakādīn vai vātavyādhīn apohati |
                            <caesura/>
                             hikkāṃ kāsamadhīmanthaṃ gulmaṃ śvāsaṃ ca dustaram || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.38">
                        <l> ṣaṇmāsān upayujyaitad antravṛddhim apohati |
                            <caesura/>
                             pratyagradhātuḥ puruṣo bhavec ca sthirayauvanaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.39">
                        <l> rājñām etad dhi kartavyaṃ rājamātrāś ca ye narāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sukhinaḥ sukumārāś ca dhaninaś cāpi ye narāḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="548" n="464a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.40">
                        <l> balākaṣāyapītebhyas tilebhyo vā 'py anekaśaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tailam utpādya ||
                            <caesura/>
                             tat kvāthaśatapākaṃ kṛtaṃ śubham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.41">
                        <l> nivāte nibhṛtāgāre prayuñjīta yathābalam | jīrṇe 'smin payasā snigdham
                            aśnīyāt ṣaṣṭikaudanam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.42">
                        <l> anena vidhinā droṇam upayujyānnamīritam | bhuñjīta dviguṇaṃ kālaṃ
                            balavarṇānvitas tataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.43">
                        <l> sarvapāpair vinirmuktaḥ śatāyuḥ puruṣo bhavet | śataṃ śataṃ tathotkarṣo
                            droṇe droṇe prakīrtitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.44">
                        <l> balākalpenātibalāguḍūcyādityaparṇiṣu | saireyake vīratarau śatāvaryāṃ
                            trikaṇṭake || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.45">
                        <l> tailāni madhuke kuryāt prasāriṇyāṃ ca buddhimān | nīlotpalaṃ varīmūlaṃ
                            gavye kṣīre vipācayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.46">
                        <l> śatapākaṃ tatas tena tilatailaṃ paced bhiṣak | balātailasya kalkāṃs tu
                            supiṣṭāṃs tatra dāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.47">
                        <l> sarveṣām eva jānīyād upayogaṃ cikitsakaḥ | balātailavad eteṣāṃ guṇāṃś
                            caiva viśeṣataḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne mūḍhagarbhacikitsitaṃ nāma pañcadaśo
                            'dhyāyaḥ ||15 ||</l>
                    </lg>

                <div n="16" subtype="adhyāyaḥ" type="level2">
                    <head>ṣoḍaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.1">
                        <l> athāto vidradhītāṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="548" n="464b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.3">
                        <l> uktā vidradhayaḥ ṣaḍye teṣv asādhyas tu sarvajaḥ | śeṣeṣvām eṣu kartavyā
                            tvaritaṃ śophavat kriyā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.4">
                        <l> vātaghnamūlakalkais tu ghṛtatailavasāyutaiḥ | sukhoṣṇo bahalo lepaḥ
                            prayojyo vātavidradhau || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.5">
                        <l> sānūpaudakamāṃsas tu kākolyādiḥ satarpaṇaḥ | snehāmlasiddho lavaṇaḥ
                            prayojyaś copanāhane || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.6">
                        <l> veśavāraiḥ sakṛśaraiḥ payobhiḥ pāyasais tathā | svedayet satataṃ cāpi
                            nirharec cāpi śoṇitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.7">
                        <l> sa ced evam upakrāntaḥ pākāyābhimukho yadi | taṃ pācayitvā śastreṇa
                            bhindyād bhinnaṃ ca śodhayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.8">
                        <l> pañcamūlakaṣāyeṇa prakṣālya lavaṇottaraiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tailair bhadrādimadhukasaṃyuktaiḥ pratipūrayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.9">
                        <l> vair ecanikayuktena traivṛtena viśodhya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             pṛthakparṇyādisiddhena traivṛtena ca ropayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.10">
                        <l> paittikaṃ śarkarālājāmadhukaiḥ sārivāyutaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             pradihyāt kṣīrapiṣṭair vā payasyośīracandanaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.11">
                        <l> pākyaiḥ śītakaṣāyair vā kṣīrair ikṣurasais tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             jīvanīyaghṛtair vā 'pi secayec charkarāyutaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.12">
                        <l> trivṛddharītakīnāṃ ca cūrṇaṃ lihyānmadhudravam |
                            <caesura/>
                             jalaukobhir hareccāsṛk pakvaṃ cāpaṭhya buddhimān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.13">
                        <l> kṣīravṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa prakṣālyaudakajena vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             tilaiḥ sayaṣṭīmadhukaiḥ sakṣaudraiḥ sarpiṣā yutaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.14">
                        <l> upadihya pratanunā vāsasā veṣṭayed vraṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāmadhukośīrapadmakaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.15">
                        <l> saharidraiḥ kṛtaṃ sarpiḥ sakṣīraṃ vraṇaropaṇam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣīraśuklāpṛthakparṇīsamaṅgārodhracandanaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.16">
                        <l> nyagrodhādipravāleṣu teṣāṃ tvakṣvathavā kṛtam |

                             sādhyeṣu tatpathyagaṇair vidadhyād ghṛtāni sekāṃś ca tathopadehān ||
                        </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.4">

                            coṣṇagaṇāḥ prayojyāḥ sekeṣu lepeṣu tathā sadantīn

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.7">

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.9">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.37">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.41">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.42">
                        <l> syan mārkavasya ca rasena nihanti tailaṃ nāḍīṃ kaphānilakṛtām apacīṃ
                            vraṇāṃś ca |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="553" n="469b"/> stanye gate vikṛtimāśu bhiṣak tu dhātrīṃ pītāṃ

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.43">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.44">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.45">
                        <l> sāmānyam etad upadiṣṭam ato viśeṣād doṣān payonipatitān śamayed
                            yathāsvam |
                            <caesura/>
                             rogaṃ stanotthitam avekṣya bhiṣag vidadhyād yad vidradhāvabhihitaṃ
                            bahuśo vidhānam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.46">
                        <l> saṃpacyamānam api taṃ tu vinopanāhaiḥ saṃbhojanena khalu pācayituṃ
                            yateta |
                            <caesura/>
                             śīghraṃ stano hi mṛdumāṃsatayopanaddhaḥ sarvaṃ prakotham upayāty
                            avadīryate ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="554" n="470a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.47">
                        <l> pakve tu dugdhahariṇīḥ parihṛtya nāḍīḥ kṛṣṇaṃ ca cūcukayugaṃ vidadhīta
                            śastram |
                            <caesura/>
                             āme vidāhini tathaiva gate ca pākaṃ dhātryāḥ stanau satatam eva ca
                            nirduhīta ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne visarpanāḍīstanarogacikitsitaṃ nāma
                            saptadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||17 || </l>
                    </lg>
                </div>

                <div n="18" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>aṣṭādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.1">
                        <l> athāto granthyapacyarbudagalagaṇḍacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.3">
                        <l> granthiṣvathāmeṣu bhiṣag vidadhyāc chophakriyāṃ vistaraśokāryāḥ
                            pittārbude kāyavirecanaṃ ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             vighṛṣya codumbaraśākagojīpatrair bhṛśaṃ kṣaudrayutaiḥ pralimpet ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.4">
                        <l> tailaṃ pibet sarpiratho dvayaṃ vā dattvā vasāṃ vā trivṛtaṃ vidadhyāt|
                            apehivātādaśamūlasiddhaṃ vaidyaścatuḥsnehamatho dvayaṃ vā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.5">
                        <l> hiṃsrā+atha rohiṇyamṛtā+atha bhargī śyonākabilvāgurukṛṣṇagandhāḥ| gojī
                            ca piṣṭā saha tālapatryā granthau vidheyo 'nilaje pralepaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.6">
                        <l> svedopanāhān vividhāṃś ca kuryāt tathā prasiddhān aparāṃś ca lepān|
                            vidārya vā pakvamapohya pūyaṃ prakṣālya bilvārkanarendratoyaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.7">
                        <l> tilaiḥ sapañcāṅgulapatramiśraiḥ saṃśodhayet saindhavasaṃprayuktaiḥ|
                            śuddhaṃ vraṇaṃ vā+apyuparopayettu tailena rāsnāsaralānvitena|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.8">
                        <l> viḍaṅgayaṣṭīmadhukāmṛtābhiḥ siddhena vā kṣīrasamanvitena| jalaukasaḥ
                            pittakṛte hitās tu kṣīrodakābhyāṃ paribecanaṃ ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.9">
                        <l> kākolivargasya ca śītalāni pibet kaṣāyāṇi saśarkarāṇi| <pb facs="555"
                                n="471a"/>drākṣārasenekṣurasena vā+api cūrṇaṃ pibeccāpi
                            harītakīnām|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.10">
                        <l> madhūkajambvarjunavetasānāṃ tvagbhiḥ pradehānavacārayeta| saśarkarair vā
                            tṛṇaśūnyakandair dihyādabhīkṣṇaṃ muculundajair vā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.11">
                        <l> vidārya vā pakvamapohya pūyaṃ dhāvet kaṣāyeṇa vanaspatīnām| tilaiḥ
                            sayaṣṭīmadhukair viśodhya sarpiḥ prayojyaṃ madhurair vipakvam|| </l>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.12">
                        <l> hṛteṣu doṣeṣu yathānupūrvyā granthau bhiṣak śleṣmasamutthite tu|
                            svinnasya vimlāpanameva kuryād aṅguṣṭhalohopalaveṇudaṇḍaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.13">
                        <l> vikaṅkatāragvadhakākaṇantīkākādanītāpasavṛkṣamūlaiḥ| ālepayet

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.14">
                        <l> amarmajātaṃ śamamaprayāntamapakvamevāpaharedvidārya| dahet sthite cāsṛji
                            siddhakarmā sadyaḥkṣatoktaṃ ca vidhiṃ vidadhyāt|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.15">
                        <l> yā māṃsakandyaḥ kaṭhinā bṛhatyastāsveṣa yojyaś ca vidhirvidhijñaiḥ|
                            śastreṇa vā++āpāṭhya supakvamāśu prakṣālayet pathyatamaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ||
                        </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.16">
                        <l>
                            <pb facs="555" n="471b"/> saṃśodhanais taṃ ca viśodhayet tu kṣārottaraiḥ
                            kṣaudraguḍapragāḍhaiḥ| śuddhe ca tailaṃ tvavacāraṇīyaṃ
                            viḍaṅgapāṭhārajanīvipakvam|| </l>
                    </lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.17">

                            hutāśataptena muhuḥ pramṛjyāllohena dhīmānadahan hitāya|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.18">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.19">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.21">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.23">

                <div n="19" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>ekonaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.1">
                        <l> athāto vṛddhyupadaṃśaślīpadacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.3">
                        <l> antravṛddhyā vinā ṣaḍyā vṛddhayas tāsu varjayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             aśvādiyānaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ veganigraham || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.4">
                        <l> atyāsanaṃ caṅkramaṇam upavāsaṃ gurūṇi ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatrādito vātavṛddhau traivṛtasnigdhamāturam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.5">
                        <l> svinnaṃ cainaṃ yathānyāyaṃ pāyayeta virecanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             kośāmratilvakair aṇḍaphalatailāni vā naram || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.6">
                        <l> sakṣīraṃ vā pibenmāsaṃ tailameraṇḍasaṃbhavam |
                            <caesura/>
                             tataḥ kāle 'nilaghnānāṃ kvāthaiḥ kalkaiś ca buddhimān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.7">
                        <l> nirūhayen nirūḍhaṃ ca bhuktavantaṃ rasaudanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             yaṣṭīmadhukasiddhena tatas tailena yojayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.8">
                        <l> snehopanāhau kuryāc ca pradehāṃś cānilāpahān |
                            <caesura/>
                             vidagdhāṃ pācayitvā vā sevanīṃ parivarjayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.9">
                        <l> bhindyāttataḥ prabhinnāyāṃ yathoktaṃ kramam ācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             pittajāyāmapakvāyāṃ pittagranthikramo hitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.10">
                        <l> pakvāṃ vā bhedayed bhinnāṃ śodhayet kṣaudrasarpiṣā |
                            <caesura/>
                             śuddhāyāṃ ca bhiṣagdadyāttailaṃ kalkaṃ ca ropaṇam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.11">
                        <l> raktajāyāṃ jalaukobhiḥ śoṇitaṃ nirhared bhiṣak |
                            <caesura/>
                             pibed virecanaṃ vā 'pi śarkarākṣaudrasaṃyutam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.12">
                        <l> pittagranthikramaṃ kuryād āme pakve ca sarvadā |
                            <caesura/>
                            <pb facs="560" n="476a"/> vṛddhiṃ kaphātmikāmuṣṇair mūtrapiṣṭaiḥ
                            pralepayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.13">
                        <l> pītadārukaṣāyaṃ ca piben mūtreṇa saṃyutam |
                            <caesura/>
                             vimlāpanād ṛte vā 'pi śleṣmagranthikramo hitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.14">
                        <l> pakvāyāṃ ca vibhinnāyāṃ tailaṃ śodhanamiṣyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             sumanāruṣkarāṅkoṭhasaptaparṇeṣu sādhitam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.15">
                        <l> medaḥsamutthāṃ saṃsvedya lepayet surasādinā |
                            <caesura/>
                             śirovirekadravyair vā sukhoṣṇair mūtrasaṃyutaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.16">
                        <l> svinnāṃ cāveṣṭya paṭṭena samāśvāsya tu mānavam |
                            <caesura/>
                             rakṣan phale sevanīṃ ca vṛddhipatreṇa dārayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.17">
                        <l> medas tataḥ samuddhṛtya dadyāt kāsīsasaindhave |
                            <caesura/>
                             badhnīyāc ca yathoddiṣṭaṃ śuddhe tailaṃ ca dāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.18">
                        <l> manaḥśilālalavaṇaiḥ siddhamāruṣkareṣu ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             mūtrajāṃ svedayitvā tu vastrapaṭṭena veṣṭayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.19">
                        <l> sevanyāḥ pārśvato 'dhastād vidhyed vrīhimukhena tu |
                            <caesura/>
                             athātra dvimukhāṃ nāḍīṃ dattvā visrāvayed bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.20">
                        <l> mūtraṃ nāḍīm athoddhṛtya sthagikābandham ācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             śuddhāyāṃ ropaṇaṃ dadyād varjayed antrahaitukīm || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.21">
                        <l> aprāptaphalakoṣāyāṃ vātavṛddhikramo hitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatra yā vaṅkhaṇasthā tāṃ dahed ardhenduvattrayā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.22">
                        <l> samyaṅmārgāvarodhārthaṃ kośaprāptāṃ tu varjayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             tvacaṃ bhittvā 'ṅguṣṭhamadhye dahec cāṅgaviparyayāt || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.23">
                        <l> anenaiva vidhānena vṛddhī vātakaphātmike |
                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.24">
                        <l> śaṅkhopari ca karṇānte tyaktvā yatnena sevanīm |

                             vyatyāsādvā sirāṃ vidhyed antravṛddhinivṛttaye || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.25">
                        <l> upadaṃśeṣu sādhyeṣu snigdhasvinnasya dehinaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sirāṃ vidhyenmeḍhramadhye pātayed vā jalaukasaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.26">
                        <l> hared ubhayataś cāpi doṣān atyartham ucchritān |
                            <caesura/>
                             sadyo 'pahṛtadoṣasya rukśophāv upaśāmyataḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.27">
                        <l> yadi vā durbalo jantur na vā prāptaṃ virecanam |
                            <caesura/>
                             nirūheṇa haret tasya doṣān atyartham ucchritān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.28">

                            <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.29">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.30">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.33">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.34">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.35">

                            kvāthābhyāṃ paribecayet|| </l>

                            prāksthānoktair hitair api|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.37">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.39">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.40">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.41">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.42">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.44">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.45ab">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.46">

                            bhasma haritālaṃ manaḥśilā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.47">

                            paś cāccheṣaṃ dahed bhiṣak|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.51">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.52">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.53">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.54">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.55">
                        <l> gulphasyādhaḥ sirāṃ vidhyecchlīpade pittasaṃbhave| pītaghnīṃ ca kriyāṃ
                            kuryāt pittārbudavisarpavat|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.56">
                        <l> sirāṃ suviditāṃ vidhyed aṅguṣṭhe ślaiṣmike bhiṣak| madhuyuktāni
                            cābhīkṣṇaṃ kaṣāyāṇi piben naraḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.57">
                        <l> pibed vā+apyabhayākalkaṃ mūtreṇānyatamena ca| kaṭukāmamṛtāṃ śuṇṭhīṃ
                            viḍaṅgaṃ dāru citrakam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.58">
                        <l> hitaṃ vā lepane nityaṃ bhadradāru sacitrakam| viḍaṅgamaricārkeṣu nāgare

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.59">

                            yavānnaṃ ca hitaṃ sadā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.60">
                        <l> pibet sarṣapatailaṃ vā ślīpadānāṃ nivṛttaye| pūtīkarañjapatrāṇāṃ rasaṃ
                            vā+api yathābalam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.61">
                        <l> anenaiva vidhānena putrañjīvakajaṃ rasam| prayuñjīta bhiṣak prājñaḥ

                    <pb facs="562" n="478a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.62">

                            peyametadbhiṣagjitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.63">
                        <l> kākādanīṃ kākajaṅghāṃ bṛhatīṃ kaṇṭakārikām| kadambapuṣpīṃ mandārīṃ
                            lambāṃ śukanasāṃ tathā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.64">
                        <l> dagdhvā mūtreṇa tadbhasma srāvayet kṣārakalpavat| tatra dadyāt
                            pratīvāpaṃ kākodumbarikārasam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.65">
                        <l> madanāc ca phalāt kvāthaṃ śukākhyasvarasaṃ tathā| eṣa kṣāras tu pānīyaḥ
                            ślīpadaṃ hanti sevitaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.66">
                        <l> apacīṃ galagaṇḍaṃ ca grahaṇīdoṣam eva ca| bhaktasyānaśanaṃ caiva hanyāt
                            sarvaviṣāṇi ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.67">
                        <l> eṣveva tailaṃ saṃsiddhaṃ nasyābhyaṅgeṣu pūjitam| etānevāmayān hanti ye
                            ca duṣṭavraṇā nṛṇām|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.68">
                        <l> dravantīṃ trivṛtāṃ dantīṃ nīlīṃ śyāmāṃ tathaiva ca| saptalāṃ śaṅkhinīṃ
                            caiva dagdhvā mūtreṇa gālayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.69">
                        <l> dadyāc ca triphalākvāthameṣa kṣāras tu sādhitaḥ| adho gacchati pītas tu
                            pūrvaiś cāpy āśiṣaḥ samāḥ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <head>viṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ|</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.1">
                        <l> athātaḥ kṣudrarogacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.2">
                        <l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.3">
                        <l> tatrājagallikāmāmāṃ jalaukobhir upācaret| śuktiśrughnīyavakṣārakalkaiś
                            cālepayed bhiṣak|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.4">
                        <l> śyāmālāṅgalakipāṭhākalkair vā+api vicakṣaṇaḥ| pakvāṃ vraṇavidhānena
                            yathoktena prasādhayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.5-6">
                        <l> andhālajīṃ yavaprakhyāṃ panasīṃ kacchapīṃ tathā| pāṣāṇagardabhaṃ caiva
                            pūrvaṃ svedena yojayet||
                            <caesura/>
                             manaḥśilātālakuṣṭhadārukalkaiḥ pralepayet| paripākagatān bhittvā
                            vraṇavat samupācaret|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.7-8">
                        <l> vivṛtāmindravṛddhāṃ ca gardabhīṃ jālagardabham| irivellīṃ gandhanāmnīṃ
                            kakṣāṃ visphoṭakāṃstathā||
                            <caesura/>
                             pittajasya visarpasya kriyayā sādhayed bhiṣak| ropayet sarpiṣā pakvān
                            siddhena madhurauṣadhaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.9-10">
                        <l> cipya(ppa)muṣṇāmbunā siktamutkṛtya srāvayed bhiṣak| cakratailena
                            cābhyajya sarjacūrṇena cūrṇayet||
                            <caesura/>
                             bandhenopacareccainamaśakyaṃ cāgninā dahet| madhurauṣadhasiddhena
                            tatastailena ropayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.11">
                        <l> kunakhe vidhirapyeṣa kāryo hi bhiṣajā bhavet| upācared anuśayīṃ
                            śleṣmavidradhivadbhiṣak|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="563" n="471a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.12-13">
                        <l> vidārikāṃ samabhyajya svinnāṃ vimlāpya lepayet|
                            nagavṛttikavarṣābhūbilvamūlaiḥ supeṣitaiḥ||
                            <caesura/>
                             vraṇabhāvagatāyāṃ vā kṛtvā saṃśodhanakriyām| ropaṇārthaṃ hitaṃ tailaṃ
                            kaṣāyamadhuraiḥ śṛtam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.14">
                        <l> pracchānair vā jalaukobhiḥ srāvyā+apakvā vidārikā| ajakarṇaiḥ
                            sapālāśamūlakalkaiḥ pralepayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.15">
                        <l> pakvāṃ vidārya śastreṇa paṭolapicumardayoḥ| kalkena tilayuktena
                            sarpirmiśreṇa lepayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.16">
                        <l> vaddhvā ca kṣīravṛkṣasya kaṣāyaiḥ khadirasya ca| vraṇaṃ prakṣālayec
                            chuddhāṃ tatastāṃ ropayet punaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.17-18">
                        <l> medo 'rbudavidhānena sādhayec charkarārbudam| kacchūṃ vicarcikāṃ pāmāṃ
                            kuṣṭhavat samupācaret||
                            <caesura/>
                             lepaś ca śasyate sikthaśatāhvāgaurasarṣapaiḥ| vacādārvīsarṣapair vā
                            tailaṃ vā naktamālajam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.19">
                        <l> sāratailamathābhyaṅge kurvīta kaṭukaiḥ śṛtam| pādadāryāṃ sirāṃ viddhvā
                            svedābhyaṅgau prayojayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.20">
                        <l> madhūcchiṣṭavasāmajjasarjacūrṇaghṛtaiḥ kṛtaḥ| yavāhvagairikonmiśraiḥ
                            pādalepaḥ praśasyate|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.21-22">
                        <l> pādau siktvā++āranālena lepanaṃ hy alase hitam| kalkīkṛtair
                            nimbatilakāsīsālaiḥ sasaindhavaiḥ||
                            <caesura/>
                             lākṣāraso 'bhayā vā+api kāryaṃ syādraktamokṣaṇam| siddhaṃ rase
                            kaṇṭakāryāstailaṃ vā sārṣapaṃ hitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.23">
                        <l> kāsīsarocanaśilācūrṇair vā pratisāraṇam| <pb facs="563" n="479b"
                            />utkṛtya dagdhvā snehena jayet kadarasaṃjñakam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.24">
                        <l> indralupte sirāṃ mūrdhni snigdhasvinnasya mokṣayet| kalkaiḥ samaricair
                            dihyācchilākāsīsatutthakaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.25-26">
                        <l> kaṭannaṭadārukalkair lepanaṃ vā praśasyate| pracchayitvā+avagāḍhaṃ vā
                            guñjākalkair muhurmuhuḥ||
                            <caesura/>
                             lepayed upaśāntyarthaṃ kuryād vā+api rasāyanam|
                            mālatīkaravīrāgninaktamālavipācitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.27-29">
                        <l> tailam abhyañjane śastam indraluptāpahaṃ param| arūṃṣikāṃ hṛte rakte
                            secayen nimbavāriṇā||
                            <caesura/>
                             dihyāt saindhavayuktena vājiviṣṭhārasena tu| haritālaniśānimbakalkair
                            vā sapaṭolajaiḥ||
                            <caesura/>
                             yaṣṭīnīlotpalair aṇḍamārkavair vā pralepayet| indraluptāpahaṃ tailam
                            abhyaṅge ca praśasyate| sirāṃ dāruṇake viddhvā snigdhasvinnasya mūrdhani
                            || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.30">
                        <l> avapīḍaṃ śirobastimabhyaṅgaṃ ca prayojayet| kṣālane
                            kodravatṛṇakṣāratoyaṃ praśasyate|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.31">
                        <l> upariṣṭāt pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ palitanāśanam|
                            <caesura/>
                             masūrikāyāṃ kuṣṭhaghnalepanādikriyā hitā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.32ab">
                        <l> pittaśleṣmavisarpoktā kriyā vā saṃpraśasyate| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="564" n="480a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.32cd-33ab">
                        <l> jatumaṇiṃ samutkṛtya maṣakaṃ tilakālakam||
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣāreṇa pradahed yuktyā vahninā vā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.33cd">
                        <l> nyacche vyaṅge sirāmokṣo nīlikāyāṃ ca śasyate|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.34">
                        <l> yathānyāyaṃ yathābhyāsaṃ lālāṭyādisirāvyadhaḥ|
                            <caesura/>
                             ghṛṣṭvā dihyāttvacaṃ piṣṭvā kṣīriṇāṃ kṣīrasaṃyutām|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.35">
                        <l> balatibalayaṣṭyāhvarajanīrvā pralepanam| payasyāgurukālīyalepanaṃ vā
                            sagairikam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.36">
                        <l> kṣaudrājyayuktayā limped daṃṣṭrayā śūkarasya ca| kapittharājādanayoḥ
                            kalkaṃ vā hitam ucyate|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.37">
                        <l> yauvane piḍakāsveṣa viśeṣācchardanaṃ hitam|
                            <caesura/>
                             lepanaṃ ca vacārodhrasaindhavaiḥ sarṣapānvitaiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.38ab">
                        <l> kustumburuvacālodhrakuṣṭharvā lepanaṃ hitam| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.38cd-39ab">
                        <l> padminīkaṇṭake roge chardayen nimbavāriṇā||
                            <caesura/>
                             tenaiva siddhaṃ sakṣaudraṃ sarpiḥpānaṃ pradāpayet| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.39cd">
                        <l> nimbāragvadhayoḥ kalko hita utsādane bhavet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.40-41">
                        <l> parivṛttiṃ ghṛtābhyaktāṃ susvinnām upanāhayet|
                            <caesura/>
                             tato 'bhyajya śanaiś carma cānayet pīḍayen maṇim||
                            <caesura/>
                             praviṣṭe ca maṇau carma svedayed upanāhanaiḥ|
                            <caesura/>
                             trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā vātaghnaiḥ sālvaṇādibhiḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.42">
                        <l> dadyād vātaharān bastīn snigdhāny annāni bhojayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             vapāṭikāṃ jayed evaṃ yathādoṣaṃ cikitsakaḥ|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.43">
                        <l> niruddhaprakaśe nāḍīṃ lauhīm ubhayato mukhīm|
                            <caesura/>
                             dāravīṃ vā jatukṛtāṃ ghṛtābhyaktāṃ praveśayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.44">
                        <l> pariṣeke vasāmajjaśiśumāravarāhayoḥ| cakratailaṃ tathā yojyaṃ
                            vātaghnadravyasaṃyutam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.45">
                        <l> tryahāttryahāt sthūlatarāṃ samyaṅnāḍīṃ praveśayet| sroto vivardhayed
                            evaṃ snigdhamannaṃ ca bhojayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.46ab">
                        <l> bhittvā vā sevanīṃ muktvā sadyaḥkṣatavadācaret|</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.46cd-47ab">
                        <l> sanniruddhagudaṃ rogaṃ valmīkaṃ vahnirohiṇīm||
                            <caesura/>
                             pratyākhyāya yathāyogaṃ cikitsitamathācaret| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.47cd">
                        <l> visarpoktena vidhinā sādhayed agnirohiṇīm|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.48ab">
                        <l> sanniruddhagude yojyā niruddhaprakaśakriyā|</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.48cd-49">
                        <l> śastreṇotkṛtya valmīkaṃ kṣārāgnibhyāṃ prasādhayet||
                            <caesura/>
                             vidhānenārbudoktena śodhayitvā ca ropayet| valmīkaṃ tu bhaved yasya
                            nātivṛddhamamarmajam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.50">
                        <l> tatra saṃśodhanaṃ kṛtvā śoṇitaṃ mokṣayed bhiṣak| kulatthikāyā mūlaiś ca
                            guḍūcyā lavaṇena ca|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.51">
                        <l> ārevatasya mūlaiś ca dantīmūlais tathaiva ca| śyāmāmūlaiḥ sapalalaiḥ
                            śaktumiśraiḥ pralepayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.52">
                        <l> susnigdhaiś ca sukhoṣṇaiś ca bhiṣak tam upanāhayet| pakvaṃ vā
                            tadvijānīyād gatīḥ sarvā yathākramam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.53">
                        <l> abhijñāya tataśchittvā pradahenmatimān bhiṣak| saṃśodhya duṣṭamāṃsāni
                            kṣāreṇa pratisārayet|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.54">
                        <l> vraṇaṃ viśuddhaṃ vijñāya ropayenmatimān bhiṣak| sumanā granthayaś caiva
                            bhallātakamanaḥśile|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.55">
                        <l> kālānusārī sūkṣmailā candanāguruṇī tathā| etaiḥ siddhaṃ nimbatailaṃ
                            valmīke ropaṇaṃ hitam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.56">
                        <l> pāṇipādopariṣṭāttu chidrair bahubhir āvṛtam| valmīkaṃ yat saśophaṃ
                            syādvarjyaṃ tat tu vijānatā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.57-58ab">
                        <l> dhātryāḥ stanyaṃ śodhayitvā bāle sādhyā+ahipūtanā|
                            paṭolapatratriphalārasāñjanavipācitam||
                            <caesura/>
                             pītaṃ ghṛtaṃ nāśayati kṛcchrāmapyahipūtanām|</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.58cd">
                        <l> triphalākolakhadirakaṣāyaṃ vraṇaropaṇam|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.59">
                        <l> kāsīsarocanātutthaharitālarasāñjanaiḥ|
                            <caesura/>
                             lepo 'mlapiṣṭo badarītvagvā saindhavasaṃyutā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.60">
                        <l> kapālatutthajaṃ cūrṇaṃ cūrṇakāle prayojayet|
                            <caesura/>
                             cikitsenmuṣkakacchūṃ cāpy ahipūtanapāmavat|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <pb facs="565" n="481a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.61">
                        <l> gudabhraṃśe gudaṃ svinnaṃ snehābhyaktaṃ praveśayet|
                            <caesura/>
                             kārayed gophaṇābandhaṃ madhyacchidreṇa carmaṇā|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.62">
                        <l> vinirgamārthaṃ vāyoś ca svedayec ca muhurmuhuḥ|
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣīre mahatpañcamūlaṃ mūṣikāṃ cāntravarjitām|| </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.63">
                        <l> paktvā tasmin pacetailaṃ vātaghnauṣadhasaṃyutam|
                            <caesura/>
                             gudabhraṃśamidaṃ kṛcchraṃ pānābhyaṅgāt prasādhayet||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.20.trailer">
                        <l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthāne kṣudrarogacikitsitaṃ nāma viṃśo
                            'dhyāyaḥ ||20||</l>
                    </lg>
                </div>
            </div>

        </body>
